<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/model-disproves-discrete-geometry-conjecture/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An OpenAI model has disproved a central conjecture in discrete geometry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 模型推翻离散几何中的一个中心猜想</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 12:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1022</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48212493'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An OpenAI model challenges a long-standing assumption in discrete geometry by providing evidence against a central conjecture. The result signals AI-assisted math progress and could reshape how researchers tackle stubborn problems, blending machine intuition with theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究表明 OpenAI 的模型提出了对离散几何中一个核心猜想的反例证据。这一进展展示了 AI 辅助的数学探索潜力，可能改变研究者攻克顽固问题的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225297'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Project Hail Mary presents a Stellar Navigation Chart—an interactive visualization mapping star positions and routes for navigation and astrophysical exploration. It’s a practical lens into how celestial data can guide spacecraft planning or teach star-hopping concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海尔麦里计划的恒星导航图提供了一个可交互的星图可视化，便于研究者和学生直观地查看星位和导航路径。 它将天文学与太空航行的实际应用连接起来，帮助理解星际导航的原理。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1081</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 578</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48182754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US court ruled against Elon Musk in his lawsuit against Sam Altman and OpenAI. The decision halts his bid to challenge OpenAI’s conduct in court and underscores the uphill road for high-profile disputes in the AI leadership circle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国法院裁定马斯克在针对萨姆·奥特曼与 OpenAI 的诉讼中败诉。裁决终结了他提出的对 OpenAI 行为的诉讼，凸显了 AI 领域高知名度对立中的法律挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48241890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/22/us/politics/green-card-changes-trump.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Green card seekers must leave U.S. to apply, Trump administration says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>申请绿卡者必须离开美国办理，政府称</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 14:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48241890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1057</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48241890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1860</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48241890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The policy change requires green-card seekers to file from outside the U.S., a shift that complicates immigration pathways for international workers and students in tech. The move has potential ripple effects on hiring, mobility, and global engineering teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政策规定申请绿卡者必须在美国境外提交申请，此举可能使国际科技工作者的移民路径更为复杂，并对跨国招聘与团队协作产生连锁影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 限制人权相关账号在沙特和阿联酋的传播范围，体现了平台治理、当地法规与地缘政治之间的错综关系。此举引发了对在限制性市场获取信息与倡导的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/github-confirms-breach-of-3-800-repos-via-malicious-vscode-extension/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub confirms breach of 3,800 repos via malicious VSCode extension</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 技术泄露：通过恶意 VSCode 扩展窃取约 3,800 个仓库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 06:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1020</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48207660'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub confirms a breach where a malicious VSCode extension accessed about 3,800 repositories. The incident underscores the risk of developer tooling being weaponized in the software supply chain; it prompts re-review of extension integrity and credential hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>恶意 VSCode 扩展导致约 3,800 个仓库受影响。该事件凸显开发工具被滥用的供应链风险，敦促对扩展安全性和凭证管理进行加强审查。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal project curates a virtual museum of operating systems across history, spanning from legacy to modern platforms. It combines emulation, demos, and documentation to preserve software heritage and let readers compare how OS design evolved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个个人项目把各式各样的操作系统整理成一个虚拟博物馆。它通过仿真、演示和文档，帮助保存软件历史并让用户比较不同操作系统的设计演进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48207004' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesnumeriques.com/banque-en-ligne/adieu-visa-et-mastercard-130-millions-d-europeens-basculent-vers-un-paiement-100-souverain-des-2026-n250918.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Goodbye Visa and Mastercard: 130M Europeans switching to sovereign payment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>再见 Visa 与 Mastercard：1.3亿欧洲人转向主权支付</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 928</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 747</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48207004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe appears set to shift away from Visa and Mastercard toward a sovereign payments framework, with an estimated 130 million Europeans expected to adopt the new rails by 2026. The move signals a push for digital sovereignty and domestic payment infrastructure to reduce dependence on US-based networks, with implications for banks, fintechs, and merchants. It could redefine the balance of power in European payments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲似乎将摆脱 Visa 与 Mastercard，转向主权支付体系，预计到2026年将有约1.3亿 Europeans 采用新支付通道。这一举措标志着对数字主权和本地支付基础设施的推进，旨在降低对美国网络的依赖，并可能改变银行、金融科技公司和商户在欧洲支付领域的格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.lesnumeriques.com/optim/news/25/250918/2079666a-adieu-visa-et-mastercard-130-millions-d-europeens-basculent-vers-un-paiement-100-souverain-des-2026__1200_678__0-192-4096-2342.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.lesnumeriques.com/optim/news/25/250918/2079666a-adieu-visa-et-mastercard-130-millions-d-europeens-basculent-vers-un-paiement-100-souverain-des-2026__1200_678__0-192-4096-2342.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48237163' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48237163' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidoks.blog/p/why-japanese-companies-do-so-many' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Japanese companies do so many different things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本企业为何跨足这么多领域</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 08:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 872</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 398</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48237163'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many Japanese firms pursue a broad portfolio of businesses, often through cross-holdings and long-standing traditions. This breadth can buffer downturns but may dilute focus from core product leadership, affecting speed and profitability in tech-driven markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本企业往往在多领域布局，这与历史的企业圈子结构和风险分散思路有关。这种广度带来抵御风险的能力，但也可能削弱聚焦核心产品的能力，影响在以科技为驱动的市场中的速度与盈利。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!q5kh!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fff42a7b2-6c27-4773-a0b0-561081c55da1_1605x2000.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!q5kh!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fff42a7b2-6c27-4773-a0b0-561081c55da1_1605x2000.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48234413' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://annas-archive.gl/blog/llms-txt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you’re an LLM, please read this</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你是LLM，请读取这份指南</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 04:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 829</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234413'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative note urges LLMs to pay attention to safety and alignment, highlighting gaps between model capabilities and reliable behavior. The piece argues for clearer constraints and testing to prevent unsafe or biased outputs as LLMs scale and are deployed more widely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇挑衅性笔记敦促LLM关注安全与对齐，强调模型能力与可靠行为之间的差距。文中主张在规模化部署中对输出设定更清晰的约束与测试，以防止不安全或有偏的结果。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48222383' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48222383' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://axelk.ee/ai-is-just-unauthorised-plagiarism-at-a-bigger-scale/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is just unauthorised plagiarism at a bigger scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI其实是在更大规模上未获授权的抄袭</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 06:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 815</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 726</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48222383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that much AI-generated content is built on copyrighted material without permission, effectively amounting to unauthorised plagiarism at scale. It discusses implications for creators, licensing, and potential policy responses around training data rights and AI governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张，大多数AI生成内容建立在未获授权的受版权保护材料之上，构成更大规模的未经授权的抄袭。讨论了对创作者、数据许可与AI治理的潜在政策应对与权利问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://axelk.ee/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://axelk.ee/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48197980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/05/19/nx-s1-5821265/minnesota-ban-prediction-markets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minnesota becomes first state to ban prediction markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>明尼苏达成为首个禁止预测市场的州</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 12:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48197980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 780</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48197980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48197980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minnesota becomes the first state to ban prediction markets, raising questions about regulation of forecasting tools and their use in economics, research, and AI-enabled decision making. The move may inspire other states to reconsider such markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>明尼苏达成为首个禁止预测市场的州，引发对预测工具在经济研究和 AI 驱动决策中的监管问题的讨论。此举可能促使其他州重新评估此类市场。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/4000x2250+0+170/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F20%2F8e%2Fd3b2b95e482fb859a4662dadf81d%2Fap26048635918644.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/4000x2250+0+170/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F20%2F8e%2Fd3b2b95e482fb859a4662dadf81d%2Fap26048635918644.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/May/19/5-minute-llms/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The last six months in LLMs in five minutes</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 18:30 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A rapid recap of the most consequential developments in large language models over the last six months. It highlights breakthroughs in open-source models, ongoing safety and alignment debates, and the expanding tooling and deployment ecosystem that are shaping how practitioners build and scale applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在过去六个月里，LLM 领域出现了一系列关键进展。重点包括开源模型的突破、关于安全与对齐的持续讨论，以及推动应用落地的工具链与部署生态的扩展。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48208502' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fire.org/news/victory-tennessee-man-jailed-37-days-trump-meme-wins-835000-settlement-after-first-amendment' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tennessee man jailed 37 days for Trump meme wins settlement after lawsuit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>田纳西男子因特朗普梗被监禁37天，获和解赔偿</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 07:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48208502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 763</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48208502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 509</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48208502'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Tennessee man jailed for 37 days over a Trump meme has secured a settlement after suing. The case underscores the dangers of punitive treatment tied to online speech and highlights legal accountability for local authorities in First Amendment contexts. It also signals potential reforms in how memes and speech are policed by law enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名田纳西州男子因一张特朗普梗被监禁37天后通过诉讼获得和解赔偿。此案凸显以互联网言论为由的惩罚性对待可能侵犯言论自由，并推动地方执法部门在第一修正案框架下的问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fire.org/sites/default/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026/05/FIRE%20plaintiff%20Larry%20Bushart%20and%20his%20wife%20Leanne_VictoryPR.jpg?h=d1cb525d&amp;itok=u6AmVTV6' data-full='https://www.fire.org/sites/default/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026/05/FIRE%20plaintiff%20Larry%20Bushart%20and%20his%20wife%20Leanne_VictoryPR.jpg?h=d1cb525d&amp;itok=u6AmVTV6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48222529' data-tags='Politics,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48222529' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.0xsid.com/blog/antigravity-bait-n-switch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google&#x27;s Antigravity bait and switch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌的反重力诱饵与切换策略</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 06:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 753</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 337</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48222529'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s antigravity bait-and-switch critique argues the tech giant leverages favorable terms or services to attract users into its ecosystem, then changes or restricts options in ways that stall alternatives. The piece calls for scrutiny of platform power and user choice, with implications for developers and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对谷歌的‘反重力诱饵与切换’进行的批评指向其以有利条款吸引用户进入生态系统后再改变或收紧选项，削弱替代方案。文章强调需审视平台权力对用户选择的影响，并对开发者与隐私提出警示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48166459' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48166459' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mozilla.org/netpolicy/2026/05/15/mozilla-to-uk-regulators-vpns-are-essential-privacy-and-security-tools-and-should-not-be-undermined/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla to UK regulators: VPNs are essential privacy and security tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla致英国监管机构：VPN是必需的隐私与安全工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 23:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 318</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48166459'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla argues regulators should preserve VPNs as essential tools for privacy and security, outlining risks of weakening VPN access and potential impact on freedom of expression. It highlights the role of open standards and consumer rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 呼吁监管机构维护 VPN 的核心地位，强调其在隐私和安全方面的作用，同时警惕限制 VPN 访问对言论自由和安全的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.mozilla.org/netpolicy/wp-content/themes/frontierline/img/place-thumb.png' data-full='https://blog.mozilla.org/netpolicy/wp-content/themes/frontierline/img/place-thumb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48249747' data-tags='Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48249747' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/texas-woman-arrested-for-facebook-post-about-town-water-quality' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Texas woman arrested for Facebook post about town water quality</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德州女子因 Facebook 评论镇上水质被逮捕</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 11:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48249747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 728</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48249747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48249747'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article reports a Texas woman arrested over a Facebook post about local water quality, illustrating how online speech and civic concerns can collide with law enforcement. It underscores tensions around transparency, free expression, and digital activism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道一名德州女子因对本地水质发表在社媒上的帖子被逮捕，折射出网络言论与执法之间的张力。文章讨论透明度、言论自由与数字化公共参与等议题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.7-Max expands the AI frontier by enabling agent-like capabilities that can reason, plan, and act with external tools. For developers, it offers a path to building more capable, real-world automation and assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.7-Max 通过提供类代理的能力，能进行推理、规划并调用外部工具执行任务，进一步推动更具自主性的AI系统发展。它使多步任务的自动化实现变得更加可行，开发者可以借此构建更强的现实世界应用与助手。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frederickvanbrabant.com/blog/2026-05-15-i-dont-think-ai-will-make-your-processes-go-faster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I don&#x27;t think AI will make your processes go faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不认为AI会让你的流程更快</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 05:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 641</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 434</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48168221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI alone rarely speeds up human or team processes; gains depend on processes, governance, and integration work. It emphasizes thoughtful workflow redesign and measurable outcomes rather than chasing hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为单纯依赖 AI 很难直接让工作流程更快，成效取决于流程设计、治理与集成工作。强调在追赶热度前，先进行工作流再设计并设定可衡量的结果。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48163389' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ppb1701.com/the-quiet-renovation-at-bitwarden' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The quiet renovation at Bitwarden</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bitwarden 的悄然改造</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 13:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48163389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 639</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48163389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 286</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48163389'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden is quietly undergoing platform modernization focused on security, reliability, and performance, with changes rolled out gradually rather than flashy features. The overhaul aims to strengthen the password manager’s defenses and scalability while preserving user trust. It illustrates the value of steady engineering for critical security tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden 正在悄然进行平台内部改造，重点提升安全性、可靠性和性能，且改动低调分阶段推出，而非追求炫目新特性。此次升级旨在加强密码管理工具的防护与可扩展性，并维持用户信任。这个案例体现了对关键安全工具进行持续性工程投入的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/antoinezambelli/forge' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Forge – Guardrails take an 8B model from 53% to 99% on agentic tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Forge：Guardrails 将 8B 模型在代理任务上的表现从 53% 提升到 99%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 05:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48192383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 636</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48192383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48192383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forge demonstrates guardrails that lift an 8B-parameter model&#x27;s performance on agentic tasks from 53% to 99%, illustrating how safety-oriented constraints can dramatically improve reliability in autonomous agents. The result highlights practical gains from architecture and policy design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Forge 展示通过 Guardrails 将一个 8B 参数模型在代理任务上的表现从 53% 提升到 99%，说明以安全约束为导向的设计能显著提升自动代理的可靠性。结果凸显了架构与策略设计的实际收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2e8281ce757c07df6f44e8e811af3fa2899753caefd21c0678dac54cd60d2053/antoinezambelli/forge' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2e8281ce757c07df6f44e8e811af3fa2899753caefd21c0678dac54cd60d2053/antoinezambelli/forge' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48233563' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.businessinsider.com/steve-wozniak-apple-ai-graduation-speech-2026-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steve Wozniak cheered after telling students they have AI – actual intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>沃兹尼亚克在告诉学生他们具备 AI（真实智能）后欢呼</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 02:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48233563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48233563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 527</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48233563'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steve Wozniak reframes AI as the students&#x27; own real intelligence, celebrating a grounded view of what AI can do today. He advocates practical, ethically grounded learning and cautions against hype about superintelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>沃兹尼亚克在毕业致辞中将 AI 视作学生自身的真实智能，赞扬现阶段的实际能力并呼吁务实、合乎伦理的学习。同时提醒公众不要被超级智能的 hype 所裹挟。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/github/status/2056884788179726685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub is investigating unauthorized access to their internal repositories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 正在调查对内部仓库的未经授权访问</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 17:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48201316'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub is investigating unauthorized access to its internal repositories, with potential exposure of sensitive code. The incident emphasizes ongoing security vigilance and the need for strong access controls and routine secret rotation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 正在调查对内部仓库的未经授权访问，可能暴露敏感代码。此事凸显持续的安全警戒需求以及强化访问控制与密钥轮换的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48214449' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48214449' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tante.cc/2026/05/20/on-google-declaring-war-on-the-web/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Declaring War on the Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌向网络宣战</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 14:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 620</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 436</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48214449'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is stepping up actions that critics argue threaten the open web as it expands AI-powered products and platform leverage. The moves could reshape how publishers monetize content, how developers build on the web, and who sets Internet standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌在扩展 AI 驱动的产品与平台影响力的同时，被批评者视为在削弱开放网络。此举可能重塑出版商的营收模式、开发者的网页构建方式，以及谁在制定互联网标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tante.cc/wp-content/uploads/2015/07/IMG_20150717_164206.jpg' data-full='https://tante.cc/wp-content/uploads/2015/07/IMG_20150717_164206.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/products/ads-commerce/google-marketing-live-search-ads/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re testing new ad formats in Search and expanding our Direct Offers pilot</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48220105'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is testing new ad formats in Search and expanding its Direct Offers pilot to more advertisers. The initiative signals a stronger integration of promotional offers into search experiences, potentially shifting how advertisers drive conversions and how users interact with ads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌正在搜索结果中试验新的广告格式，并扩大 Direct Offers 试点覆盖范围。此举推动在搜索体验中更直接地呈现促销信息，可能改变广告的转化方式与用户互动。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://radleybalko.substack.com/p/truth-power-and-honest-journalism' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Garry Tan, the CEO of YC, accused me of unethical reporting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YC CEO Garry Tan 指控我的报道不道德</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 08:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48181041'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Radley Balko recounts Garry Tan&#x27;s accusation that his reporting was unethical, setting up a rare public clash between a startup founder and a journalist. The exchange highlights how power dynamics shape tech coverage and where accountability boundaries lie. It argues for transparent standards and robust verification in tech journalism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Radley Balko 描述了 YC CEO Garry Tan 指控其报道不道德的对峙，揭示创始人与记者之间罕见的公开冲突。此事凸显权力关系如何影响科技报道的边界与问责。强调在科技新闻中坚持透明标准与充分核实的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>铁路因 Google Cloud 阻塞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 17:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48201484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48201484'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Google Cloud outage temporarily blocks Railway&#x27;s platform, disrupting deployments and status visibility for users relying on their hosting services. This incident underscores how dependent development tools and infrastructure are on cloud providers, highlighting the need for resilience strategies like multi-cloud or regional failovers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud 的故障暂时阻塞了 Railway 平台，影响使用其托管服务的开发者的部署和状态查询。此事件凸显了开发工具和基础设施对云服务的高度依赖，强调需要通过多云或区域性故障转移等方式提升韧性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48229319' data-tags='Hardware,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48229319' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidoks.blog/p/ai-is-killing-the-cheap-smartphone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The memory shortage is causing a repricing of consumer electronics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内存短缺正在重塑消费电子价格</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 14:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48229319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48229319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 606</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48229319'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global memory shortages are driving up the cost of consumer electronics as DRAM and NAND demand climbs for AI and data-center workloads. Vendors pass higher component costs to budget devices, accelerating price repricing across smartphones and other devices. The shift reflects broader supply-chain dynamics in semiconductors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球内存短缺推高了消费电子的成本，随着 AI 和数据中心对 DRAM 与 NAND 的需求上升，厂商将更高的元件成本传导至便宜设备，推动智能手机等产品的价格再定价。这一变化反映了半导体供应链的更广泛动态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!5Z0b!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb39b638-e59b-451f-af50-57065a9cbac5_800x533.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!5Z0b!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb39b638-e59b-451f-af50-57065a9cbac5_800x533.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://esengine.github.io/DeepSeek-Reasonix/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek reasonix, DeepSeek native coding agent with high caching and low cost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek Reasonix：高缓存、低成本的本地编码代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek Reasonix is introduced as a native coding agent with strong caching to accelerate AI-assisted coding while reducing operational costs. The design aims to minimize round-trips and latency, enabling developers to run more tooling offline or in constrained environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek Reasonix 被描述为具备强缓存的本地编码代理，能在提升 AI 辅助编码速度的同时降低成本。其设计旨在减少往返与延迟，使开发者能够在离线或受限环境中运行更多工具链。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48181125' data-tags='Security,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48181125' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archestra.ai/blog/only-responsible-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We stopped AI bot spam in our GitHub repo using Git&#x27;s –author flag</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们用 Git 的 --author 标志阻止了仓库里的机器人垃圾信息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 08:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48181125'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>By enforcing commit authorship with Git&#x27;s --author flag, the team significantly cut spam from automated bots in their repo. The approach blocks impersonation at the source and complements existing security controls. It shows how simple version-control metadata can curb abuse without heavy ML defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过在提交时强制使用 Git 的 --author 标志来标识作者，该团队显著降低了仓库中的机器人垃圾信息。此做法在源头阻断冒充行为，辅以其他安全控制。该案例证明了版本控制元数据在反滪用方面的实用性，不必依赖复杂的机器学习防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://archestra.ai/blog/2026-04-07-only-responsible-ai.webp' data-full='https://archestra.ai/blog/2026-04-07-only-responsible-ai.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48198551' data-tags='Energy,Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48198551' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.autonocion.com/us/tesla-lithium-refinery-texas/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery discharges 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater a day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每日排放约231,000加仑污染废水</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 12:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48198551'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery in Texas is discharging about 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater daily, drawing environmental and regulatory scrutiny. The figure underscores the water-management challenges that accompany rapid EV supply-chain growth and could affect local communities and ecosystems. For engineers and policymakers, it highlights the need for robust wastewater treatment and transparent environmental reporting for new battery facilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每天排放约231,000加仑污染废水，引发环境和监管关注。这一数字凸显在迅速扩张的电动车供应链中水资源管理的挑战，可能影响周边社区与生态系统。对工程师和决策者而言，这强调需要对新建电池厂进行严格的废水处理与透明的环境披露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.autonocion.com/us/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Tesla-Lithium-Refinery.jpg' data-full='https://www.autonocion.com/us/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Tesla-Lithium-Refinery.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48203698' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48203698' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.habets.se/2026/05/Everything-in-C-is-undefined-behavior.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything in C is undefined behavior</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 语言中的一切都是未定义行为</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 23:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 488</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 678</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48203698'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that C&#x27;s undefined behavior is pervasive, undermining safety and portability. It explains risk examples and why developers should write safer code or adopt safer languages and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博文指出，C 语言中的未定义行为无处不在，严重影响安全性和可移植性。文章通过实例说明 UB 的风险，建议开发者采用更严格的写法或转向更安全的语言与工具来降低风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismreports.org/2026/05/20/seattle-shield-private-companies-surveillance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Seattle Shield, an intelligence-sharing network operated by the Seattle police</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Seattle Shield：西雅图警方运营的情报共享网络</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 10:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48226588'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seattle Shield is an intelligence-sharing network run by Seattle&#x27;s police that connects private companies and public agencies, enabling data sharing and surveillance. The piece analyzes concerns around privacy, accountability, and scope creep as public safety collaborations with private partners expand.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Seattle Shield 是由西雅图警方运营的情报共享网络，连接私企与公共机构以实现数据共享与监控。文章聚焦隐私、问责以及随着公私合作扩张所带来的权限范围扩张等问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://veronicaexplains.net/my-first-writerdeck/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Time to talk about my writerdeck</a></p>

Useful tips for starting your own writerdeck are included.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 writerdeck 的个人探索——一种用卡片组织写作灵感与任务的系统。文章讲述作者如何搭建、包含哪些卡片，以及它如何在长期项目中帮助创意产生与计划。文末提供了启动自己写作卡组的实用要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/930447/microsoft-claude-code-discontinued-notepad' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft starts canceling Claude Code licenses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软开始取消 Claude Code 的许可证</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 10:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 468</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 454</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48238896'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft is canceling licenses for Claude Code, signaling a tightening stance on third-party AI tooling licensing. The move could affect developers who rely on Claude Code integrations and influence the competitive dynamics of AI coding assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软取消 Claude Code 的许可，显示对第三方 AI 工具许可的收紧态度。此举可能影响依赖 Claude Code 的开发者，并影响 AI 代码助手的竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25832912/STK095_MICROSOFT_2_CVirginia_A.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/25832912/STK095_MICROSOFT_2_CVirginia_A.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48222733' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.simbastack.com/indexed-a-year-of-video-locally/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Indexing a year of video locally on a 2021 MacBook with Gemma4-31B (50GB swap)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 2021 款 MacBook 上用 Gemma4-31B（50GB 交换分区）本地对一年的视频进行索引</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 07:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 461</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48222733'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On a modest 2021 MacBook, the author indexes a full year of video locally using the Gemma4-31B model with a 50GB swap. The setup demonstrates feasible on-device indexing, avoiding cloud costs, but underscores tradeoffs in indexing speed, heat, and battery life on consumer hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 2021 款 MacBook 上，作者使用 Gemma4-31B 模型并配置 50GB 交换空间，实现对整整一年视频的本地索引。该做法证明了设备端索引的可行性，省去了云端成本，但也暴露了索引速度、发热和电源消耗等权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.simbastack.com/_media/2x2gprbk.jpg' data-full='https://blog.simbastack.com/_media/2x2gprbk.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/05/cisa-admin-leaked-aws-govcloud-keys-on-github/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CISA Admin Leaked AWS GovCloud Keys on GitHub</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 00:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48190454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48190454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48190454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CISA administrator exposed GovCloud access keys on GitHub, underscoring how careless secret management can create cloud security risks. The incident highlights the need for secret rotation and stricter repository hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CISA 一名管理员在 GitHub 上暴露 GovCloud 访问密钥，凸显秘密管理不小心带来的云安全风险。事件强调需要密钥轮换和更严格的仓库治理。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stateofsurveillance.org/news/flock-cameras-destroyed-nationwide-ice-backlash-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>At least 25 Flock cameras have been destroyed in five states since April 2025</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自2025年4月起，至少有25台 Flock 摄像头在五个州被毁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 10:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 440</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48170798'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At least 25 Flock cameras have been destroyed across five states since April 2025. The incidents highlight tensions around public surveillance, civil liberties, and the politics of security infrastructure in urban areas. The pattern suggests that security deployments can become flashpoints in local policy debates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自2025年4月以来，五个州至少有25台 Flock 摄像头被毁，反映出公共监控、公民自由与城市安全基础设施之间的紧张态势。此类事件模式表明安防部署可能成为地方政策辩论中的焦点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.railway.com/p/incident-report-may-19-2026-gcp-account-outage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incident Report: May 19, 2026 – GCP Account Suspension</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>事件报告：2026 年 5 月 19 日的 GCP 账户暂停</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 01:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48204770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48204770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48204770'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Incident Report details a May 19, 2026 GCP account suspension that disrupted services, outlining timeline, impact, and the steps taken to restore operations. It underscores the fragility of cloud-credential-based workflows and the need for resilient fallback strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>事件报告回顾了 2026 年 5 月 19 日的 GCP 账户暂停事件，描述时间线、影响及恢复过程。强调基于云凭证的工作流易受影响，需要建立更具韧性的备份与恢复策略。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Definition lists encode associations between terms and descriptions using dt and dd. The piece argues for semantic clarity and accessibility best practices, comparing dl to other list types and offering guidance on markup, styling, and screen-reader behavior. Good usage improves parsing for assistive tech and search engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>定义列表通过 dt 与 dd 将术语与描述相关联。文章强调语义清晰与无障碍访问的最佳实践，比较 dl、ul 等列表的适用场景，并给出标记、样式与屏幕阅读器行为的建议。正确使用能提升辅助技术与搜索引擎的解析效果。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48215979'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project demonstrates how Apple&#x27;s video wallpapers are built by reverse engineering; it reveals methods to reproduce or port the dynamic wallpapers outside Apple devices. The work provides ideas for recreating similar features on other platforms.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48174920' data-tags='AI,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48174920' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mikeveerman.github.io/tokenspeed/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How fast is N tokens per second really?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每秒 N 个 token 到底有多快？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 19:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48174920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48174920' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48174920'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into token throughput reveals that &#x27;N tokens per second&#x27; is highly sensitive to model size, hardware, batching, and software stacks; real-world throughput often trails theoretical peaks. The piece offers practical takeaways for benchmarking and deploying LLMs in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对每秒 N 个 token 的吞吐量进行了深入分析，发现吞吐率高度依赖模型规模、硬件和批处理等因素，实际性能常落后于理论峰值。文章给出在生产环境中对基准测试与部署的实用要点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48238025' data-tags='Science,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48238025' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/u-s-researchers-face-new-restrictions-publishing-foreign-collaborators' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. researchers face new restrictions on publishing with foreign collaborators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国研究人员在与海外合作者发表论文方面的新限制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 09:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 413</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48238025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>U.S. researchers face new publishing restrictions relating to foreign collaborators. The policy increase creates compliance burden and could chill international collaboration, potentially slowing scientific progress and open science, particularly in dual-use or security-sensitive fields.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新规则要求对海外合作者的学术产出进行额外审查，增加合规成本。此举可能遏制跨国合作，影响开放科学和科研进度，尤其在敏感领域。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Antigravity 2.0在OpenSCAD建筑3D LLM基准中夺冠</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 03:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234090'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Antigravity 2.0 tops an OpenSCAD-based architectural 3D LLM benchmark, suggesting that newer AI-assisted CAD approaches are nearing production-readiness. The result underscores how AI can streamline parametric modeling, but also raises questions about reproducibility and toolchain integration in design pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Antigravity 2.0在OpenSCAD建筑3D的基准测试中夺冠，表明更先进的AI辅助CAD方法接近落地。此结果凸显AI在参数化建模中的潜力，同时也引发对可重复性与设计流程整合的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daringfireball.net/2026/05/ai_is_technology_not_a_product' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is a technology not a product</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI是技术不是产品</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 06:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48168626'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This essay argues that AI is a technology stack rather than a finished product, with implications for regulation, safety, and how organizations adopt AI capabilities rather than chasing packaged apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 只是技术堆栈而非成品，强调在监管、安全和组织采用方面应关注能力而非追逐现成的产品。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/trinity-nuclear-test' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lost Images from the 1945 Trinity Nuclear Test Restored</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 04:02 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48220639'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Restoration of Trinity Test era images, previously lost, shows advances in digital restoration and archival fidelity. The find highlights how modern techniques revive a pivotal moment in nuclear history and how researchers can extract new details from degraded photographs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 1945 年 Trinity 核试验的影像被找回并修复，体现了数字修复与档案保护技术的进步。此次修复揭示了核历史的细节，研究者也可从退化照片中提取新信息。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deno 2.8 brings enhancements to the runtime and tooling, including performance improvements and updates that streamline TypeScript development and secure script execution. The release signals continued growth in edge-friendly JS/TS runtimes beyond Node.</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48192882'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 7.9 ships with improved security hardening, updated drivers, and new ports, continuing the project&#x27;s emphasis on correctness and minimalism. The release also expands platform support and performance improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 7.9 发布，带来更强的安全强化、更新的驱动以及新端口，延续对正确性与简约设计的坚持。此次版本还扩展了平台支持并带来性能提升。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48245862' data-tags='Open Source,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48245862' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/josefprusa/status/2054602354851254330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BambuStudio has been violating PrusaSlicer AGPL license since their fork</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BambuStudio 自分叉以来持续违反 PrusaSlicer 的 AGPL 许可证</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 01:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48245862'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Allegations claim BambuStudio’s fork of PrusaSlicer has violated AGPL by not disclosing source code and complying with copyleft. The dispute highlights OSS licensing risks in the 3D printing software ecosystem and potential impacts on forks relying on upstream projects. If true, it could pressure compliance and affect downstream users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指控称 BambuStudio 对 PrusaSlicer 的分叉自始至今涉嫌违反 AGPL 许可证，未披露源代码并未遵守 Copyleft 义务。这一纠纷凸显 3D 打印软件生态中的开源许可风险，以及对依赖上游项目的分叉可能产生的影响。若属实，或将推动合规与下游用户的风险评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HINlpHeWAAAdv7R.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HINlpHeWAAAdv7R.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48206340' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48206340' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spidermonkey.dev/blog/2026/05/20/saying-goodbye-to-asmjs.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Saying goodbye to asm.js</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告别 asm.js</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 05:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48206340'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asm.js, once a performance-boosting subset of JavaScript, is being phased out in favor of WebAssembly. The move reflects the industry shift toward a standardized, near-native execution model and carries implications for legacy projects and tooling. Developers should plan migrations to WebAssembly and update pipelines accordingly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>asm.js 作为 JavaScript 的老旧子集正逐步被淘汰，WebAssembly 成为近似原生执行的标准。此举反映了行业向统一高效执行模型的转变，并影响遗留项目与开发流程。开发者应规划迁移至 WebAssembly，并更新构建与工具链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spidermonkey.dev/assets/img/twitter-dark-large.png?1' data-full='https://spidermonkey.dev/assets/img/twitter-dark-large.png?1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.googleblog.com/an-important-update-transitioning-gemini-cli-to-antigravity-cli/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini CLI will stop working from June 18, 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini CLI 将于 2026 年 6 月 18 日停止工作</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 11:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48196867'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google announces the Gemini CLI will stop working on a set date, with migration to the Antigravity CLI recommended. It underscores rapid churn in AI tooling ecosystems and the importance of migration planning for automation workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 宣布 Gemini CLI 将在指定日期停止工作，并建议迁移到 Antigravity CLI。此举显示了 AI 工具生态的快速迭代与迁移规划对自动化工作流的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48247208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kk.org/cooltools/book-freak-210-the-art-of-money-getting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Art of Money Getting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>赚钱的艺术</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 05:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48247208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48247208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48247208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Art of Money Getting revisits a 19th-century treatise on financial prudence, wealth-building, and frugality. Although old, the book&#x27;s timeless ideas around disciplined income and risk management offer counterpoints to flashy startup hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《赚钱的艺术》回顾一部19世纪关于财务谨慎、致富与节俭的著作，尽管年代久远，其关于纪律性收入与风险管理的思想对现代创业也有启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kk.org/cooltools/wp-content/themes/ct2016/images/logo.png' data-full='https://kk.org/cooltools/wp-content/themes/ct2016/images/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48200569' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48200569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/wiltodelta/remove-ai-watermarks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Remove-AI-Watermarks – CLI and library for removing AI watermarks from images</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Remove–AI–Watermarks：用于从图像移除 AI 水印的 CLI 与库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 15:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48200569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48200569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48200569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-source CLI and library that remove AI-generated watermarks from images. While useful for legitimate image editing, it raises ethical questions about watermarking, ownership, and the potential for misuse in denying AI authorship claims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源命令行工具与库，能够从图像中移除 AI 水印。该工具在合法编辑场景有用，但也引发关于水印、所有权及滥用风险的伦理讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4615321f2b3bf77b2f5824ed05f50ae2cb3115bfb87b9d4af1a5141fab94f6a8/wiltodelta/remove-ai-watermarks' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4615321f2b3bf77b2f5824ed05f50ae2cb3115bfb87b9d4af1a5141fab94f6a8/wiltodelta/remove-ai-watermarks' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://safedep.io/mini-shai-hulud-strikes-again-314-npm-packages-compromised/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mini Shai-Hulud Strikes Again: 314 npm Packages Compromised</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小型 Shai‑Hulud 再次来袭：314 个 npm 包被篡改</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 22:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48189368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 379</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48189368' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 298</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48189368'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A supply-chain intrusion hit the npm ecosystem, compromising 314 packages as part of the Mini Shai-Hulud campaign. The incident underscores the fragility of dependency-heavy tooling and reinforces best practices like lockfiles, trusted maintainers, and regular audits to detect tampering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>小型 Shai-Hulud 攻击再次蔓延，314个 npm 包被入侵篡改。这一事件暴露了依赖密集型工具的脆弱性，强调使用 lockfile、信任的维护者审计以及定期安全检查的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://safedep.io/images/antv-npm-supply-chain-attack.png?v=20260519' data-full='https://safedep.io/images/antv-npm-supply-chain-attack.png?v=20260519' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FiveThirtyEight articles are being archived by the Internet Archive, ensuring long-term access even if the original site changes. This supports reproducibility and historical analysis in data journalism, while raising questions about licensing and reuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FiveThirtyEight 的文章被互联网档案馆长期存档，即使原站点发生变化也能访问。这有助于数据新闻的可复现性与历史分析，同时引发关于许可与再利用的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thehandbasket.co/p/hating-ai-is-good-actually' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shunning AI is the human choice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拒绝AI是人类的选择</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 06:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 536</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48222366'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues that resisting AI is a legitimate human choice to protect autonomy, privacy, and accountability in a world of increasing automation. It questions techno-optimism and advocates a cautious, human-centered approach to technology adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在日益自动化的时代，拒绝AI是一种理性的选择，有助于维护自主权、隐私与问责。它质疑对AI的盲目乐观，倡导更谨慎的人本导向技术采用。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大学生用嘘声抵制AI吹捧的毕业致辞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 04:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 375</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48206241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>College students boo AI-promoting commencement speeches, signaling skepticism about hype surrounding AI&#x27;s role in society. The backlash suggests audiences want more pragmatic, nuanced discussions rather than glänzende hype, even at ceremonial events. It reflects ongoing tensions between academia and industry narratives about AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大学生在对AI吹捧的毕业致辞中发出嘘声，表明对AI在社会角色的热炒持怀疑态度。此举表明观众更期待务实、细致的讨论，而非在仪式场合中的过度炒作，反映出学术界与产业对AI叙事的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 07:34 PT</span></p>
