</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48223362'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freenet is proposed as a peer-to-peer platform for decentralized apps, offering a way to run software beyond centralized app stores. It faces practical questions around performance, security, and app discovery in real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Freenet 是一个去中心化应用的点对点平台，旨在绕过集中式应用商店运行应用。 在现实场景中，它仍面临性能、安全性和应用发现等实际问题。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 270</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Andon Labs 展示了让 AI 运营广播电台的尝试，AI 负责选曲并进行自动化主持。该项目探索媒体领域自动化的边界，以及在叙事与选曲方面仍需人工介入的部分。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48177785' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/former-google-ceo-booed-graduation-speech-ai-rcna345585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eric Schmidt speech about AI booed during graduation</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eric Schmidt’s AI-themed graduation remarks were booed, signaling skepticism about the AI hype among students. The moment reflects broader tensions between industry optimism and concerns about automation&#x27;s impact on jobs and society.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>施密特在毕业典礼上就 AI 的讲话遭遇嘘声，显示学生群体对 AI 热潮的怀疑情绪。此刻折射出行业乐观情绪与对自动化对就业及社会影响的担忧之间的紧张。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48258684' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://epoch.ai/data-insights/ai-chip-component-cost-shares' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memory has grown to nearly two-thirds of AI chip component costs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 芯片成本中内存占比近三分之二</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 09:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48258684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48258684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48258684'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New cost data show memory accounts for about two-thirds of AI chip component costs, making memory the dominant design and pricing driver as models grow. This shifts emphasis to memory bandwidth, efficiency, and novel memory tech, as chips compete on how well they feed large AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新成本数据表明，内存约占 AI 芯片组件成本的三分之二，成为主导的设计和定价驱动力。随着模型规模增大，厂商更关注内存带宽、效率以及新型内存技术，以提高对大规模 AI workload 的供给能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://epoch.ai/assets/images/data-insights/ai-chip-component-cost-shares/t-ai-chip-component-cost-shares.png' data-full='https://epoch.ai/assets/images/data-insights/ai-chip-component-cost-shares/t-ai-chip-component-cost-shares.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48202047' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48202047' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260515-the-1950s-blunder-which-causes-mass-hay-fever-in-japan' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan is gripped by mass allergies. A 1950s project is to blame</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本大规模花粉过敏归咎于1950年代的一项计划</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 18:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48202047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48202047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48202047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decades-old project in the 1950s is linked to today&#x27;s mass allergy problem in Japan. The article traces how early experiments altered pollen ecosystems or health outcomes and why legacy decisions matter for science policy and public health. It highlights how long tail effects from past research can shape present-day living conditions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本因1950年代的一项研究计划而引发的花粉过敏问题在今日仍有显著影响。文章追溯早期实验如何改变花粉生态或健康结果，以及历史决策对科学政策和公共卫生的意义，强调过去研究的长期影响会塑造当下的生活环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nly7ch.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nly7ch.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48179732' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48179732' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/cyber-frontier-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Glasswing: what Mythos showed us</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Glasswing 项目：Mythos 给我们的启示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 06:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48179732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48179732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48179732'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mythos demonstrates a new approach to cybersecurity tooling with Glasswing, showcasing how frontier models can assist threat hunting and incident response. The project illustrates how AI models integrated with domain-specific data can accelerate cognitive tasks in security operations, while highlighting limits like data sensitivity and model hallucination. It signals a trend: domain-specific AI tooling for cybersecurity is moving from concept to practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mythos 的 Glasswing 展示了将前沿模型应用于网络安全工具的新思路，覆盖从威胁狩猎到事件响应的场景。该项目凸显了将 AI 模型与领域数据结合以提升安全运维认知任务的潜力，同时也提醒数据敏感性与模型可信性等局限。预示着以安全领域为目标的 AI 工具正从概念走向落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/4ILuGDGHs7n6fbasZ54VOx/0dbf7f7099880643bb53eafceb695281/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__35_.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/4ILuGDGHs7n6fbasZ54VOx/0dbf7f7099880643bb53eafceb695281/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__35_.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/email/wham-launch-005-elephant-2-p/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI has a multiplying effect on existing technical skills</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI对现有技术技能具有放大效应</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 06:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48235526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48235526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48235526'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI amplifies existing technical skills by accelerating discovery and automation, turning familiar workflows into higher-leverage activities. The takeaway is to align learning with tools that complement human judgment and to emphasize skills that scale with AI, like system design and critical thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能放大了现有技术能力，提升了发现速度和自动化水平，将熟练的工作流变成更高杠杆的活动。核心建议是将学习聚焦于与AI协同的技能，如系统设计与批判性思维等，形成可与AI共成长的能力结构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/opengraph-image.png?cac0cc658da9fd03' data-full='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/opengraph-image.png?cac0cc658da9fd03' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48197995' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48197995' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.emmi.ai/news/mistral-ai-acquires-emmi-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral AI acquires Emmi AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral AI 收购 Emmi AI 打造领先的 AI 技术栈</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 12:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48197995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48197995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48197995'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI acquires Emmi AI to assemble an end-to-end AI stack, combining models, tooling, and deployment capabilities to accelerate product development and enterprise use. The acquisition signals consolidation in the AI tooling ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 收购 Emmi AI，意在打造端到端的 AI 技术栈，整合模型、工具与部署能力，加速产品开发与企业落地。此举标志着 AI 工具生态的整合加速。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260519-google-tackles-attempts-to-hack-its-ai-results' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google’s AI is being manipulated. The search giant is quietly fighting back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌的 AI 正在被操控，搜索巨头悄然反击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 03:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48205782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48205782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48205782'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article reports ongoing attempts to manipulate Google&#x27;s AI-powered search results and describes how Google is quietly defending against manipulation with model hardening, policy updates, and improved signals. It underlines the fragility of search integrity in the age of AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章报道有针对 Google AI 搜索结果的操控尝试，描述了 Google 如何通过模型加强、策略更新和信号改进等方式进行防御。强调在 AI 时代，搜索结果的完整性更容易受到影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/advancing-content-provenance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI Adopts Google&#x27;s SynthID Watermark for AI Images with Verification Tool</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 采用 Google 的 SynthID 图像水印并提供验证工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 12:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI integrates Google&#x27;s SynthID watermark into AI-generated images and ships a verification tool to prove AI origin. This marks a shift toward verifiable content provenance and could influence platform policies, authentication tooling, and public trust in AI-generated media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Google 的 SynthID 图像水印嵌入 AI 生成的图像，并提供验证工具以证明来源。这标志着对内容可验证出处的重视，有望影响平台策略、认证工具和公众对 AI 生成媒体的信任。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/05/more-than-340-local-news-outlets-are-limiting-the-internet-archives-access-to-their-journalism/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>News outlets are limiting the Internet Archive’s access to their journalism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更多新闻机构限制互联网档案馆访问其新闻内容</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 09:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>More than 340 local news outlets are limiting the Internet Archive&#x27;s access to their journalism, raising concerns about long-term preservation, research access, and the sustainability of preserving local reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>超过 340 家地方新闻机构限制互联网档案馆对其新闻内容的访问，引发关于长期保存、研究可及性以及地方报道可持续性的担忧。文章讨论版权、授权与数字新闻生态系统中对存档访问的阻碍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Internet-Archive-server-close-up-700x452.jpg' data-full='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/Internet-Archive-server-close-up-700x452.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/stephenlthorn/auto-identity-remove' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Auto-identity-remove – Automated data broker opt-out runner for macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Auto-identity-remove——用于 macOS 的自动化数据经纪人退出工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 04:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48178184'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Auto-identity-remove is an open-source macOS tool that automates opt-out requests to data brokers, aiming to reduce personal data exposure. It demonstrates a practical approach to privacy hygiene with automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Auto-identity-remove 是一个开源的 macOS 工具，自动化向数据经纪人提交退出请求，旨在降低个人数据暴露。它展示了通过自动化实现隐私卫生的实用方法。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48183579' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.haiku-os.org/t/my-haiku-arm64-progress/19044?page=2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Haiku OS runs on M1 Macs now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Haiku OS 现可在 M1 Macs 上运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 11:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183579'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Haiku OS, a BeOS-inspired open-source system, now demonstrates running on Apple Silicon M1 Macs, marking progress in ARM64 support for this project. The update highlights the ongoing porting efforts and the challenges of booting modern Macs with niche operating systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Haiku OS 作为 BeOS 启发的开源系统，现已在 Apple Silicon 的 M1 Mac 上运行，标志着其 ARM64 支持的进展。该进展凸显了持续的移植工作以及在现代 Mac 硬件上引导小众操作系统所面临的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini Omni</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini Omni 是一个多模态、多领域的通用模型，整合工具、推理与多模态输入，体现谷歌在打造全栈 AI 助手上的努力。它强调记忆、工具使用和跨领域能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/18/world/americas/actually-democracy-dies-in-hr.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Actually, democracy dies in H.R.</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>民主其实死在 HR 里</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 07:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48180091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48180091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48180091'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that HR processes can quietly undermine democratic norms within organizations, through centralized control, surveillance, and opaque decision-making. It connects these patterns to broader concerns about democracy and calls for greater employee participation and governance safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出人力资源流程可能通过集权、监控和隐性决策等方式侵蚀组织内部的民主原则，与更广泛的民主健康相关。呼吁加强员工参与、治理保障与透明度提升。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.williamangel.net/blog/2026/05/17/offline-llm-energy-use.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Silicon costs more than OpenRouter</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 05:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 317</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48168198'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparison of energy efficiency and cost when running offline LLMs shows that consumer Apple Silicon may incur higher energy use or cost than purpose-built OpenRouter setups. Practical implications for on-device AI planning and hardware strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对离线运行的 AI 模型进行能耗与成本比较，表明 Apple Silicon 在离线推理上可能不如专用的 OpenRouter 设置高效或经济。为在设备端部署 AI 的硬件策略提供实际启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.williamangel.net/blog/2026/05/17/2026-05-17-offline-llm-energy-use.png.' data-full='https://www.williamangel.net/blog/2026/05/17/2026-05-17-offline-llm-energy-use.png.' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48226944' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48226944' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.barebones.com/products/bbedit/bbedit16.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BBEdit 16</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BBEdit 16 版</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 11:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48226944'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BBEdit 16 modernizes a long-running macOS text editor with refreshed workflows and improved editing tools, including better handling of large files and built-in Git integration. It signals continued value for desktop, local-workflow developers who prefer precise, private tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BBEdit 16 对长期存在的 macOS 文本编辑器进行了现代化升级，提升了编辑工作流、对大型文件的处理能力，并强化了与 Git 的集成。 这一更新强调了在注重本地工作流的开发者与作者群体中，桌面工具仍具备不可替代的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.barebones.com/assets/images/barebones_logo@2x.png' data-full='https://www.barebones.com/assets/images/barebones_logo@2x.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48254309' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48254309' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adaptivesupport.amd.com/s/question/0D5Pd00001YQLdMKAX/why-is-vivado-20261-dropping-linux-support-for-free-tier-?language=en_US' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is Vivado 2026.1 dropping Linux support for free tier?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Vivado 2026.1 版本不再为免费版提供 Linux 支持？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 21:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48254309'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vivado 2026.1 will drop Linux support for its free tier, cutting off Linux users from the no-cost FPGA design tool. The change pushes hobbyists, students, and small teams toward paid options or alternative ecosystems, highlighting the fragility of vendor-supplied open tooling. The move raises questions about accessibility and cross-platform support in critical design software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vivado 2026.1 将不再为免费版提供 Linux 支持，意味着 Linux 用户将无法使用这款无成本的 FPGA 设计工具。此变动可能迫使业余爱好者、学生和小团队转向付费版本或寻找替代方案，凸显了依赖厂商的跨平台开工具的脆弱性。此举引发关于可访问性与跨平台支持在关键设计软件中的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48188787' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48188787' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tuhs.org/pipermail/tuhs/2026-May/033748.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Peter Neumann has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>彼得·诺伊曼去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 20:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48188787' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48188787' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48188787'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Peter G. Neumann, a longtime observer and author on computer security risks, has died. His work on computer-related risks shaped how engineers approach safety, reliability, and policy in software and networks. His writings and talks continue to influence risk management practices in software and cybersecurity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>彼得·诺伊曼是一位长期关注并著作 Computer Related Risks 的计算机科学家，其关于计算机安全风险的工作影响了工程师对软件与网络安全、可靠性与政策的思考。他的著作和演讲继续影响软件与网络安全领域的风险管理实践。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48175140' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48175140' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.noemamag.com/there-is-no-hard-problem-of-consciousness/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It is time to give up the dualism introduced by the debate on consciousness</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>放弃意识辩论中引入的二元论</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 19:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48175140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48175140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 736</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48175140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Philosophical piece urging readers to drop the traditional mind–body dualism invoked by debates about consciousness. It argues for a monist or non-dual framework that treats consciousness as emergent from physical processes, challenging long-standing dichotomies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文呼吁放弃在意识辩论中常见的二元论思维，主张以更贴近物理过程的单一框架来理解意识。通过将意识视为物理过程的涌现，挑战长期存在的二分法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noemamag.imgix.net/2026/05/Noema_FlorianMeissner_2.jpg?fit=crop&amp;fm=pjpg&amp;h=628&amp;ixlib=php-3.3.1&amp;w=1200&amp;wpsize=noema-social-facebook&amp;s=1967d5f91d8af468ce455230916d2bcd' data-full='https://noemamag.imgix.net/2026/05/Noema_FlorianMeissner_2.jpg?fit=crop&amp;fm=pjpg&amp;h=628&amp;ixlib=php-3.3.1&amp;w=1200&amp;wpsize=noema-social-facebook&amp;s=1967d5f91d8af468ce455230916d2bcd' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/nottombrown/status/2057194829986300375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic is expanding to Colossus2. Will use GB200</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 13:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214017' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic expands to Colossus2 with GB200, signaling a push for larger AI compute resources. The move underscores ongoing scaling needs for safety-focused AI research and production workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 将扩展至 Colossus2，并将使用 GB200，这体现了对更大规模AI算力的需求。此举可能推动更大模型的研发与部署。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48258015' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://audiomass.co/?multitrack=1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Audiomass – a free, open-source multitrack audio editor for the web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Audiomass——一个免费开源的网页多轨音频编辑器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 08:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48258015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48258015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48258015'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Audiomass is a free, open-source multitrack audio editor that runs entirely in your browser. It offers a simple timeline and essential editing tools with no installation, making quick editing and sharing easy for web users. Being open-source, it invites community contributions and transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Audiomass 是一个免费、开源的浏览器端多轨音频编辑器，完全在浏览器中运行，无需安装即可完成编辑与导出。它提供简单的时间线和基本编辑工具，便于快速处理和分享音频，社区贡献也更易参与。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://audiomass.co/icon.jpg' data-full='https://audiomass.co/icon.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 21:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 287</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48254475'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on a four-year run with Amazon Web Services and the reasons behind its termination.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48231319' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48231319' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mathworks.com/company/aboutus/founders/clevemoler.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cleve Moler has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>克里夫·莫勒去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 19:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48231319'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cleve Moler, co-founder of MathWorks and the creator of MATLAB, has died. His work helped democratize numerical computing and shaped how engineers and scientists learn linear algebra and numerical methods through accessible software. His influence extends across education and industry alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MathWorks 联合创始人兼 MATLAB 的创始人之一克里夫·莫勒去世。他的工作让数值计算走进更多人手中，并通过易用的软件改变了工程和科学家学习线性代数与数值方法的方式，其影响覆盖教育与工业界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cursor.com/blog/composer-2-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cursor Introduces Composer 2.5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cursor 推出 Composer 2.5</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 10:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182516' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cursor released Composer 2.5, adding improvements to the AI agent toolchain, such as better task orchestration, reliability, and debugging. The update helps developers build more capable agents with fewer edge-case issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cursor 发布了 Composer 2.5，带来改进的 AI 代理工具链，如更好的任务编排、稳定性与调试能力。更新帮助开发者构建更强大的代理，减少边缘情况问题。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48216016' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48216016' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/doc/design/typography/1980-knuth.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Letter S, by Donald Knuth (1980) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>唐纳德·克努斯的字母 S（1980）[PDF]</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 16:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48216016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48216016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48216016'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Donald Knuth&#x27;s 1980 essay on the letter S dives into typography, shape, and legibility, reflecting the craft behind printed text. It remains a touchstone for typographers and designers alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1980 年，Knuth 的《字母 S》探讨了字形、可读性和排版技艺，揭示了印刷文字背后的匠心。该文成为字体设计领域的经典参考。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@breid.at/ultra-pure-quantum-crystals-from-an-abandoned-mine-in-a-mysterious-desert-93cc87d12314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I found ultra-pure quantum crystals in an abandoned mine in the Atacama desert</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在阿塔卡马沙漠的废弃矿井中发现超纯量子晶体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 20:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48165797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48165797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48165797'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ultra-pure quantum crystals were reportedly found in an abandoned mine in the Atacama Desert, raising questions about material provenance for quantum tech. Such crystals could provide stable substrates for quantum experiments and sensors, but their rarity highlights broader supply-chain challenges in quantum hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在阿塔卡马沙漠的废弃矿井里发现超纯量子晶体的经历引发了关于量子技术材料来源的讨论。此类晶体若用于量子比特或高纯度基底，可能提升设备稳定性，但也暴露了极端环境下材料获取与证据记录的重要性。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://carette.xyz/posts/who_will_buy_your_services/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who will buy your services if you fire us all?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你把我们全都解雇，谁来买你的服务？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 14:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48185789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 269</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48185789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48185789'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that aggressive outsourcing or mass layoffs undermine long-term customer value, as vendors risk losing the relationship, trust, and capability to deliver. It stresses sustainable staffing and human-centric service as a competitive differentiator.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，过度裁员或大规模外包会削弱长期客户价值，因为失去关系、信任与交付能力。强调以人本、可持续的人员配置提供服务，避免因削减成本而损害客户信任。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court awarded $19.5 million in a default judgment against Anna&#x27;s Archive and issued a global domain takedown order. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院对安娜的档案站作出1950万美元的默认判决，并发出全球域名下架令。此案展示了版权方可动用的强力执法手段来关闭海盗型域名，以及此类跨境执法在程序公正和全球范围内的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disassembling the 80386 microcode sheds light on how early x86 processors implemented instructions and durations. <p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla Solar Roof is on life support as it pivots to traditional panels; the premium roof product has faced cost and installation hurdles, pushing the company to focus on conventional PV. This shift may accelerate adoption of standard panels and reshape expectations for building-integrated photovoltaics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉太阳能屋顶在成本与安装难题面前陷入难题，转而重点推进传统太阳能板。此举或将加速传统面板的普及，并改变建筑一体化光伏的市场预期。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub disclosed a security compromise affecting its systems, highlighting ongoing risks for developers relying on the platform. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 披露其系统遭遇安全妥协，凸显开发者对该平台的安全依赖仍面临的风险。此事件强调在现代软件开发环境中持续强化访问控制、监控与快速事件响应的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how Kagi Search works for users with low vision, highlighting accessible UI decisions, contrast, and keyboard navigation. It offers practical tweaks and reflections on how search UX can better serve users with visual impairments.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/22/trump-mobile-confirms-it-exposed-customers-personal-data-including-phone-numbers-and-home-addresses/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump Mobile exposed customers&#x27; personal data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Trump Mobile 暴露客户个人数据</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 09:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48237684'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trump Mobile confirmed a data exposure affecting customers, including phone numbers and home addresses. The incident underscores how telecom providers and related services can leak sensitive PII, fueling risks of doxxing and phishing until proper patches and incident response are in place. Regulators and customers are watching how the company responds and remedies potential harm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Trump Mobile 确认发生数据暴露，涉及客户的电话、家庭地址等敏感信息。此事件凸显电信及相关服务在数据处理上的风险，可能被用于钓鱼或身份暴露，需尽快修补并完善事件响应机制。监管和用户将关注公司如何应对并降低潜在损害。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kanbots.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source Kanban desktop app that runs parallel agents on every card</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源看板桌面应用：每张卡片并行运行代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 11:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48239413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48239413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A desktop open-source Kanban app runs parallel agents on every card, enabling automation on tasks. This architecture lets each card spawn lightweight AI agents to perform actions like drafting, data gathering, or reminders, potentially changing how teams automate workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该桌面看板应用在每张卡片上并行运行代理，实现对任务的自动化处理。卡片级代理可执行草拟内容、信息采集或提醒等任务，或将工作流自动化推向新水平。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48216278' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/20/intuit-to-lay-off-over-3000-employees-to-refocus-on-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intuit to lay off over 3k employees to refocus on AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Intuit 将裁员超过 3000 人以聚焦 AI</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 17:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48216278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48216278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48216278'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intuit to lay off more than 3,000 employees to pivot toward AI, signaling a broader trend of AI-driven transformation among software firms. The layoffs may affect product teams and reshape internal priorities around automation and data products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Intuit 将裁员超过3000人以聚焦AI，显示软件公司向AI驱动的转型趋势。这一举措可能影响产品团队并重塑对自动化与数据产品的内部优先级。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2261740845.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2261740845.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48243863' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48243863' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isaiprofitable.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is AI Profitable Yet?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 真的盈利了吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 19:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48243863'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>While AI spend is up, there’s no universal profitability yet. ROI hinges on how AI is integrated into core processes, data quality, and the ability to monetize outcomes; many initiatives struggle with compute costs, data handling, and governance. For tech leaders, the message is to prove business value with concrete metrics before scaling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管 AI 投入增加，但尚无普遍的盈利结论。投资回报取决于 AI 与核心流程的整合、数据质量以及成果变现的能力；许多项目仍受制于算力成本、数据管理与治理。对技术领袖而言，应以具体指标证明业务价值再进行放量。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48156186' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48156186' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexplescan.com/posts/2025/08/16/kvm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two computers, one monitor, zero fiddling (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两台电脑，一块显示器，零干扰切换（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 15, 2026 / 19:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48156186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48156186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48156186'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A KVM-based setup lets you share a single monitor between two computers with seamless switching and minimal fuss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 KVM 的设置实现两台电脑共用一个显示器，切换无感、使用成本极低。文章给出优化双机工作流的实用技巧，包括键鼠共享及潜在的延迟权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48221915' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48221915' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/a-bipartisan-amendment-would-end-police-license-plate-tracking-nationwide/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Bipartisan Amendment Would End Police License Plate Tracking Nationwide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两党拟议法案将全面禁止警方车牌追踪</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 05:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48221915'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bipartisan amendment aims to end nationwide police license plate tracking, curbing the use of plate readers and limiting cross-agency data sharing. The move addresses privacy concerns while weighing how data-driven policing should be regulated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两党拟议的修正案旨在全面禁止警方车牌追踪，限制车牌识别设备的使用与跨机构数据共享。此举在关注隐私的同时，也在思考数据驱动的治安执法应如何监管。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a0e0f8f6174d8e9dcf1157d/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/security_flock_GettyImages-2271667850.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a0e0f8f6174d8e9dcf1157d/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/security_flock_GettyImages-2271667850.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qz.com/samsung-chip-workers-bonus-ai-profits-052126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung chip workers will get an average $340k bonus as AI profits soar</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>随着 AI 盈利飙升，三星芯片工人将获均薪34万美元奖金</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 18:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48230892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48230892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48230892'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung is awarding average bonuses around $340k to chip-workers as AI-driven profits rise. The payout illustrates how AI demand is boosting semiconductor margins and how companies reward critical manufacturing roles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 相关利润上升，三星芯片部门工人将获得约34万美元的奖金，凸显 AI 需求提升对半导体利润的拉动，以及企业对关键制造岗位的激励。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/lifeandstyle/2026/may/22/experience-found-baby-subway-now-26-year-old-son' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Experience: We found a baby on the subway – now he&#x27;s our 26-year-old son</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在地铁捡到的婴儿，如今已成为我们26岁的儿子</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 00:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48245571'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A family recounts finding a baby on the subway and the decades-long path that led to that child becoming their son at 26.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家人在地铁里偶遇婴儿，经过数十年的发展，这个孩子如今成了他们的儿子。故事揭示了亲情的不可预测性以及陌生情感如何在出人意料的时刻孕育成家庭。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48222241' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/may/20/how-much-companies-spend-fight-unions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US employers spend more than $1.5B a year to fight labor unions, report finds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国雇主每年花超过15亿美元打压工会，报告显示</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 06:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48222241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48222241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new report tallies more than $1.5 billion spent annually by U.S. employers to oppose labor unions. The figures reflect extensive lobbying, legal strategies, and HR tactics that shape workers’ organizing power and wage growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新报告统计美国雇主每年在打压工会上的花费超过15亿美元。数字揭示了广泛的游说、诉讼与人力资源策略，这些做法直接影响工会动员力和薪资增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/fac585ae7acb9910dba1eb32e0c3b11f24fa8e56/240_0_2391_1913/master/2391.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=5353f4b3b4ecece7ad35314e05ade57b' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/fac585ae7acb9910dba1eb32e0c3b11f24fa8e56/240_0_2391_1913/master/2391.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=5353f4b3b4ecece7ad35314e05ade57b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idahonews.com/news/local/two-f-18-fighter-jets-have-crashed-during-an-airshow-at-mountain-home-air-force-base' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two EA-18 fighter jets collide at Mountain Home airshow, pilots ejected safely</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>山地家园空军基地航空表演中两架 EA-18 战斗机相撞，飞行员均安全弹出</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 14:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48173468'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two EA-18 fighter jets collided during an air show at Mountain Home AFB, with both pilots ejecting safely. The incident will trigger an official investigation and could affect future safety procedures at civilian-military air shows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Mountain Home 空军基地的航空表演中，两架 EA-18 战斗机相撞，飞行员均安全弹出。事件将启动官方调查，可能影响未来军民航空表演的安全措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://idahonews.com/resources/media2/16x9/922/1320/100x1/90/c2f82f89-feb1-4f16-a22d-5b2789a14048-Crash1.jpg' data-full='https://idahonews.com/resources/media2/16x9/922/1320/100x1/90/c2f82f89-feb1-4f16-a22d-5b2789a14048-Crash1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/05/22/microsoft-ai-cost-problem-tokens-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft reports AI is more expensive than paying human employees</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软称 AI 的成本高于雇佣人工</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 20:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48244434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48244434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48244434'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft reports that in some cases, running AI—tokens, agents, and infrastructure—costs more than employing people. This underscores the tricky economics behind AI-powered workflows and products, where cost discipline, model efficiency, and pricing matter just as much as accuracy. It explains why enterprises push for cheaper tokens, better caching, and smarter orchestration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软在某些情形下称 AI 的运行成本（包括 token、代理与基础设施）高于雇佣人力的成本。这凸显了 AI 驱动工作流的经济学挑战——成本控制、模型效率与定价与准确性同等重要。因此企业倾向于追求更便宜的 token、更高效的缓存和更聪明的编排。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2262969436-e1779468619700.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2262969436-e1779468619700.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://temertymedicine.utoronto.ca/news/how-decades-sleep-research-led-new-sleep-apnea-drug' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sleep research led to a new sleep apnea drug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>睡眠研究催生新型睡眠呼吸暂停药物</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 15:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48242278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48242278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decades of sleep research pinpointed targets for sleep apnea therapies, leading to a novel drug that improves nighttime breathing. The translational path from basic sleep science to therapeutics demonstrates how understanding sleep physiology can yield tangible medical advances. If approved, the drug could add a new option for patients who struggle with current treatments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数十年的睡眠研究锁定了睡眠呼睡眠暂停治疗的新靶点，从而催生了一种改善夜间呼吸的新药。将基础睡眠科学转化为治疗手段，展示了对睡眠生理学的深入理解如何带来切实的医学进步。若获批，该药或为现有治疗无效的患者提供新的治疗选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewlock.net/exploring-the-dotnet-11-preview-2-dotnet-gets-union-types/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>.NET (OK, C#) finally gets union types</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.NET（C#）引入联合类型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 05:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234954'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>.NET 11 preview 2 adds union types to C#, enabling sum types and more expressive data modeling. This brings functional-style patterns to C# and could simplify error handling and domain modeling. Expect changes to pattern matching and code that previously relied on discriminated unions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>.NET 11 预览版为 C# 引入联合类型，使表达变体和不同类型的值更为简洁，从而提升数据建模和错误处理的能力。这将把函数式风格的模式匹配带入 C#，并可能影响依赖判定的联合类型的代码。相关代码与库可能需要调整以利用新特性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andrewlock.net/content/images/2026/unions.png' data-full='https://andrewlock.net/content/images/2026/unions.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48256912' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Back End Code Generation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约束衰退：后端代码生成中 LLM 代理的脆弱性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 05:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48256912'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues that LLM-based agents degrade in reliability when performing backend code generation due to constraint drift, brittle tool integration, and context limits. It discusses implications for building robust AI copilots and practical engineering safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文指出，在后端代码生成任务中，基于 LLM 的代理因约束漂移、工具集成脆弱性和上下文容量限制而表现不稳。并讨论构建鲁棒 AI 助手以及落地工程保护的要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48212992' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48212992' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/not-alive-not-dead-disembodied-human-brains-used-drug-testing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Not alive, but not dead: disembodied human brains used for drug testing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>非活而未死：用于药物测试的离体人脑</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 12:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48212992'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers are using disembodied human brain tissue in drug-testing experiments, challenging the line between life and death. The approach could yield new data on how drugs affect brain tissue, but raises ethical concerns about consent and the moral status of the tissue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在药物测试中使用离体的人脑组织，这模糊了生死界线。此做法可能带来关于脑组织对药物反应的新数据，但也引发知情同意与脑组织道德地位的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48212279' data-tags='Business,Science,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48212279' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.beyondplastics.org/press-releases/starbucks-cups-recyclable-report' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracking Starbucks&#x27; &#x27;widely recyclable&#x27; cups: none ended up at recycling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追踪星巴克‘广泛可回收’杯子：没有进入回收体系</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 11:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48212279'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigation tracking Starbucks&#x27; &#x27;widely recyclable&#x27; cups finds none were recycled, highlighting a gap between green marketing and real-world recycling outcomes. The report suggests green claims may outpace waste management capabilities and calls for transparent reporting and stronger standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对星巴克‘广泛可回收’杯子的调查发现，几乎没有杯子真的被回收。此现象揭示了绿色宣传与实际回收之间的差距，呼吁建立更强的回收体系并对企业可持续承诺进行透明披露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5eda91260bbb7e7a4bf528d8/5f7235fbe1caec3fa0c89c1a/6a0b5e6b3e8a107497c8a001/1779312913141/Beyond%2BPlastics%2B-%2Bblue%2Bmin%2Bround%2B-%2Bweb%2B1080px.png?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5eda91260bbb7e7a4bf528d8/5f7235fbe1caec3fa0c89c1a/6a0b5e6b3e8a107497c8a001/1779312913141/Beyond%2BPlastics%2B-%2Bblue%2Bmin%2Bround%2B-%2Bweb%2B1080px.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48166744' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48166744' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://polypad.amplify.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Polypad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Polypad：在线数学绘板</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 00:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48166744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48166744'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polypad is an online math sandbox that lets users manipulate tiles, shapes, and equations to explore geometry and algebra interactively. It supports collaborative learning and could serve as a versatile classroom or self-study tool.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Polypad 是一个在线数学沙盒工具，提供可拖拽的磁砖、几何形状和方程等，便于师生在浏览器中进行协作式、动手式的数学探索。它体现了教育科技在浏览器端提供直观学习体验的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://polypad.amplify.com/images/og-tile.png' data-full='https://polypad.amplify.com/images/og-tile.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48198127' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/05/19/dumb-ways-for-an-open-source-project-to-die.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dumb ways for an open source project to die</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让开源项目死去的愚蠢之道</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 12:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48198127'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay outlining common missteps that doom open-source projects—opaque governance, dwindling maintainer energy, and misaligned incentives. It offers practical reminders for maintainers and communities to design sustainable processes and governance to avoid burnout and abandonment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章列出导致开源项目夭折的常见误区，如治理不清、维护者热情衰退、激励错位等，并给维护者与社区提出可执行的可持续治理建议，帮助避免倦怠与放弃。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48178692' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/05/18/linus-torvalds-says-ai-powered-bug-hunters-have-made-linux-security-mailing-list-almost-entirely-unmanageable/5241633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux security mailing list &#x27;almost unmanageable&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 安全邮件列表几乎难以管理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 05:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48178692'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linus Torvalds says AI-powered bug hunters have made the Linux security mailing list almost unmanageable due to volume and noise. The note highlights the tension between openness and signal quality in security discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>托瓦尔兹表示 AI 驱动的漏洞猎人让 Linux 安全邮件列表几乎难以管理，原因是信息量大且噪声较多。这体现了开放性与信息质量之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/234872.jpg?imageId=234872&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/234872.jpg?imageId=234872&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jank-lang.org/blog/2026-05-08-optimization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jank now has its own custom IR</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 15, 2026 / 10:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48151251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48151251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jank 现推出自定义中间表示（IR），提升了代码优化的灵活性并简化后端目标对接。这一举措标志着编译器基础设施走向成熟。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://corrode.dev/learn/migration-guides/go-to-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating from Go to Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Go 迁移到 Rust</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 11:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48259808'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical migration guide helps teams decide when to port Go code to Rust, with stepwise strategies, interoperability patterns, and clear trade-offs. It emphasizes balancing safety and performance gains against cost and team readiness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迁移指南从评估时机、分步迁移模式、互操作性等角度，帮助团队将代码从 Go 迁移到 Rust。强调在性能与内存安全收益、成本以及团队准备之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://corrode.dev/learn/migration-guides/go-to-rust/social.png' data-full='https://corrode.dev/learn/migration-guides/go-to-rust/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48194645' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/retail-consumer/kindle-loyalists-scramble-amazon-turns-page-old-e-readers-2026-05-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kindle loyalists scramble as Amazon turns page on old e-readers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kindle 忠实用户抢购，亚马逊停止对部分旧款电子书阅读器的支持</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 08:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48194645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48194645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48194645'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon is phasing out support for older Kindle devices, triggering a scramble among loyal customers who rely on legacy hardware. The move underscores how platform lifecycles influence device obsolescence, upgrade incentives, and the ongoing tension between infrastructure resilience and product segmentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊结束对部分老款 Kindle 的支持，引发忠实用户的抢购与换机热潮。此举反映了平台生命周期对设备淘汰、升级激励以及对产品分段策略的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/ryangrim/status/2055992439031185782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta deletes popular 1M follower account after Kuwaiti request</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 在科威特请求后删除了1M粉丝的热门账号</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 10:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48170810'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta deleted a popular account with about a million followers following a Kuwaiti government request, illustrating platform compliance with state demands. The move spotlights censorship, geopolitics, and platform accountability in a global social web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 在科威特政府请求后删除了一个拥有约一百万粉丝的热门账号，凸显平台对政府要求的合规性。这一举动引发对全球社交网络中文章审查、地缘政治与平台问责的讨论。</p>
