</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48237422' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48237422' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paulgraham.com/winc.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to convert between wealth and income tax</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>财富与所得税之间的转换方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48237422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 667</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48237422'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Graham&#x27;s piece discusses the conceptual shift between wealth and income taxation, highlighting incentives and policy implications. It offers a framework for thinking about how wealth creation interacts with tax policy and personal planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇保罗·格雷厄姆的文章探讨了财富税与所得税之间的关系及激励与政策含义，提供了理解财富创造与税制、个人规划之间关系的框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.turbifycdn.com/aah/paulgraham/bel-7.gif' data-full='https://s.turbifycdn.com/aah/paulgraham/bel-7.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48234547' data-tags='Business,Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48234547' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libertas.software/en/knowledge-hub/19/the-companies-cutting-headcount-for-ai-will-lose-to-the-ones-who-didnt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Companies Cutting Headcount for AI Will Lose to the Ones Who Didn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 AI 裁员的公司将输给那些没有裁员的公司</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 04:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firms cutting headcount to adopt AI risk losing long-term competitiveness, as AI alone won’t replace skilled work. Those who maintain or retrain staff while deploying AI tend to outperform. The piece cautions against using cost-cutting as a substitute for strategic AI adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为引入 AI 而裁员的企业可能在长期竞争力上处于不利地位，因为 AI 无法完全取代高技能工作。那些保留或培训员工并结合 AI 的公司往往表现更好。文章警示，单纯以裁员降成本不能替代对 AI 的战略性应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.libertas.software/public/blogs/19-large.webp' data-full='https://www.libertas.software/public/blogs/19-large.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48247737' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48247737' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/security-news-this-week-fbi-license-plate-reader-real-time-access/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The FBI Wants &#x27;Near Real-Time&#x27; Access to US License Plate Readers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦调查局希望实现对车牌识别系统的‘近实时’访问</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 06:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48247737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48247737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48247737'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FBI seeks near real-time access to license plate reader data to bolster investigations, raising civil liberty concerns about surveillance overreach. The debate centers on balancing security benefits with privacy protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FBI 希望获得对车牌识别系统的近实时访问以强化调查，挥之不去的监控自由担忧随之而来。讨论聚焦于在提升安全与保护隐私之间取得平衡。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48245362' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/spanish-court-declines-to-fine-nordvpn-over-laliga-piracy-blocking-order/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spanish court declines to fine NordVPN over LaLiga piracy blocking order</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙法院拒绝就 NordVPN 处以罚款</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 23:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48245362'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Spanish court refused to fine NordVPN for LaLiga piracy blocking orders, suggesting that the enforcement of blocking requests against VPNs remains legally nuanced. The decision affects how anti-piracy measures are applied to privacy-focused services and could influence future court rulings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙法院裁定不对 NordVPN就LaLiga 阻止盗版的拦截令处以罚款，显示对 VPN 服务的取证与执行仍具法理上的复杂性。此判决或将影响将来对隐私保护服务的版权拦截措施及司法走向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://torrentfreak.com/images/grass-soccer.jpg' data-full='https://torrentfreak.com/images/grass-soccer.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48230049' data-tags='Business,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48230049' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zeroshot.bearblog.dev/google-is-shattering-under-its-own-weight-the-ibm-ification-of-google/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The IBM-ification of Google?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌的 IBM 化？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 16:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48230049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48230049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48230049'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Google’s growth and complexity risk slipping into IBM-like bureaucratic inertia, examining organizational structure, decision bottlenecks, and the tension between speed and large-scale AI experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为谷歌的快速成长和日益复杂的组织结构可能陷入类似 IBM 的官僚化，探讨了组织架构、决策瓶颈以及高效运营与大规模 AI 实验之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zeroshot.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://zeroshot.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48173962' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hellmood.111mb.de//wake_up_16b_writeup.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WriteUp: 16 Bytes of x86 that turn Matrix rain into sound</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>写作笔记：16 字节的 x86 将矩阵雨变成声音</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 16:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48173962'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A write-up demonstrates how 16 bytes of x86 code can synthesize audible sound from the Matrix rain visualization, illustrating extreme code-golf techniques and low-level audio synthesis. The result highlights how tiny snippets can produce multimedia.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇写作展示了用仅仅16字节的 x86 代码就能把矩阵雨可转成声音，体现了极简编码与多媒体合成的极限。对于低级编程与代码高球爱好者而言，这是一个有趣的演示，展示了紧凑代码如何实现音频合成。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.metalevel.at/prolog/horror' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prolog Coding Horror</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Prolog 编程的恐惧</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 14:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48173268'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prolog has a reputation for a steep learning curve and quirky behavior that makes debugging a headache. This piece highlights Prolog&#x27;s backtracking and logic-first mindset, arguing many developers find it a painful fit for real-world projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文直陈 Prolog 的学习曲线陡峭、行为怪异且回溯机制复杂，调试常常令人头疼。文章聚焦其逻辑驱动的工作方式，认为在实际项目中，许多开发者会因此感到深刻的“编码恐惧”。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.metalevel.at/prolog/videos/t_antipatterns.png' data-full='https://www.metalevel.at/prolog/videos/t_antipatterns.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48211409' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usni.org/magazines/naval-history/2025/december/missile-genius-america-lost-and-china-gained' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qian Xuesen: The missile genius America lost and China gained (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>钱学森：美国失去的导弹天才，中国获得的机会（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 10:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48211409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48211409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48211409'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qian Xuesen, the renowned missile scientist, left the United States and later steered China&#x27;s rocket program. The piece revisits how his departure shaped China’s ballistic missile capabilities and the broader Cold War tech rivalry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>钱学森被誉为中国导弹之父，因在美遭遇怀疑而返回祖国，随后主导中国的火箭与导弹计划。本文回顾他离美如何助推中国导弹技术崛起，以及对冷战科技对峙的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fosdem.org/2026/schedule/event/AGWUVH-mercurial-aint-you-dead-yet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mercurial, 20 years and counting: how are we still alive and kicking? [video]</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 15, 2026 / 04:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48147351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48147351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48147351'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercurial celebrates two decades of existence, highlighting its tooling philosophy, performance, and the ongoing debate with Git. The video likely surveys the community, use cases, and what keeps Mercurial relevant in modern workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercurial 已运行二十年，展示其工具设计哲学、性能与在与 Git 的持续对比中的定位。视频回顾社区、典型场景，以及令 Mercurial 在现代工作流中仍具生命力的因素。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48238351' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/22/you-can-no-longer-google-the-word-disregard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can no longer Google the word &#x27;disregard&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你现在无法在谷歌中搜索单词“disregard”</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 09:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48238351'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky update shows Google search behavior around the word &#x27;disregard&#x27; behaving in an unexpected way, illustrating how search indexing and edge cases can alter even simple queries. It sparks reflection on search quality and the fragility of algorithmic results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于单词“disregard”的搜索表现出现意外行为，揭示搜索索引在边缘情况下可能如何改变简单查询，值得反思搜索质量与算法结果的脆弱性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48225489' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48225489' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/uk-news/2026/may/21/london-mayor-sadiq-khan-blocks-met-police-deal-with-palantir' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>London Mayor Blocks Palantir</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伦敦市长因隐私担忧阻止 Palantir 合同</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 09:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225489'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>London&#x27;s mayor blocks a Palantir deal with the Met Police over privacy and civil liberties concerns. The decision highlights tensions between data-enabled policing and public accountability, potentially slowing adoption of external analytics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伦敦市长因隐私与公民自由担忧，阻止警察部队与 Palantir 的合作。此举凸显数据驱动治安与公民权利之间的紧张关系，或影响对外部分析平台的采用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/58263c476c5d9d478efd0bb1b825a7b2744d09ed/335_78_1195_956/master/1195.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=a9e2df08d62bdaecc67c94674a7d2ab0' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/58263c476c5d9d478efd0bb1b825a7b2744d09ed/335_78_1195_956/master/1195.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=a9e2df08d62bdaecc67c94674a7d2ab0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48243293' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/news/kash-patels-apparel-site-is-trying-to-trick-visitors-into-installing-malware' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FBI director&#x27;s Based Apparel site has been spotted hosting a &#x27;ClickFix&#x27; attack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Based Apparel 网站被发现承载 ClickFix 攻击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 17:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48243293'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers spotted a &#x27;ClickFix&#x27; attack used by visitors to trick them into installing malware on Based Apparel’s site, exploiting social-engineering-like prompts. It illustrates how attackers abuse trusted brands or pages to deliver infections, even when security-conscious sites are involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究人员发现 Based Apparel 网站成为 ClickFix 攻击的载体，利用访客中的社交工程式提示诱导安装恶意软件。这一事件说明攻击者会利用受信任的品牌或页面来传播感染，即便目标网站具备一定的安全防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/03FWX7wsMyKmI8Pt0HrumDG-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1779401296.jpg' data-full='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/03FWX7wsMyKmI8Pt0HrumDG-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1779401296.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek plans to lock in a permanent 75% discount on its flagship AI model, a move that could reshape competitive dynamics and accessibility. The strategy signals a push to broaden adoption among startups and researchers.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://olano.dev/blog/dangerously-skip/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>-​-dangerously-skip-reading-code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>危险地跳过阅读代码</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48246232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A candid take on the hazards of skipping code reading—bugs, security flaws, and fragile designs creep in when teams skim rather than read. The post advocates deliberate reading, thorough reviews, and supportive tooling to catch subtle issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>直言不讳地指出跳过仔细阅读代码的风险：草率浏览、潜在的安全漏洞和脆弱的设计容易被忽视。文章主张认真阅读、全面评审，并借助工具提升代码理解与质量控制。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ftc.gov/news-events/news/press-releases/2026/05/shutterstock-pay-35-million-settle-ftc-allegations-over-illegal-subscription-cancellation-practices' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shutterstock to pay $35M over hard-to-cancel subscriptions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shutterstock 因难以取消的订阅被罚款 3500 万美元</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 12:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48184635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48184635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48184635'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shutterstock has agreed to pay $35 million to settle FTC allegations that its subscription practices made cancellations difficult and misled consumers. The case highlights regulatory scrutiny of online subscriptions and the need for clear, user-friendly cancellation flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shutterstock 同意支付 3500 万美元以解决 FTC 对其难以取消订阅做法的指控。此案凸显对在线订阅的监管关注以及提升取消流程透明度与易用性的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48167890' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48167890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Diolinux/PhotoGIMP' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Photo GIMP – A Patch for GIMP 3 for Photoshop Users</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hengefinder is described as a tool to determine when the Sun aligns with a street&#x27;s orientation, enabling urban planning, photography, and historical astronomy studies. <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48242648' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dontquotetheai.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t just paste the AI at me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别只是把 AI 的结果塞给我</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 15:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48242648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48242648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48242648'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It argues for treating AI as a component in a broader workflow, not a drop-in paste of outputs. The piece advocates clear prompts, guardrails, and measurable outcomes to avoid sloppy adoption and hallucination.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文主张将 AI 视为工作流中的一个组成部分，而非直接塞给用户的输出。强调需有清晰的提示、治理边界和可衡量的结果，避免草率采用与输出幻觉等问题。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48219918' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/helvesec/rmux' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Rmux – A programmable terminal multiplexer with a Playwright-style SDK</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Rmux——具备 Playwright 风格 SDK 的可编程终端多路复用器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 02:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48219918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48219918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48219918'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rmux is a programmable terminal multiplexer powered by a Playwright-style SDK, enabling automated terminal sessions and CLI workflows. It exposes scriptable control over panes, sessions, and commands, unlocking reproducible end-to-end testing and automation for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rmux 是一个可编程的终端多路复用器，具备 Playwright 风格的 SDK，使你能够自动化终端会话和 CLI 工作流。它提供对分屏、会话和命令的脚本化控制，帮助开发与测试人员实现可重复的端到端场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/874201033537ca21b3a3551fb0d0c717e4621b9c25c3e4d90f849c0f276d6196/Helvesec/rmux' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/874201033537ca21b3a3551fb0d0c717e4621b9c25c3e4d90f849c0f276d6196/Helvesec/rmux' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48231938' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slumber.lucaspickering.me' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slumber a TUI HTTP Client</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Slumber：一个 TUI HTTP 客户端</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 21:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48231938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slumber is a terminal-based HTTP client with a text UI designed for quick API exploration. It combines keyboard-driven navigation, editable requests, and formatted responses to streamline API testing in a lightweight, locally contained tool. The project aims to make everyday API work feel more like interacting with a native app.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Slumber 提供一个基于终端的 HTTP 客户端，配备文本界面，便于快速探索 API。它支持键盘操作、请求编辑和格式化输出，让 API 测试更像在本地应用中进行，从而提升工作效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://slumber.lucaspickering.me/images/demo.gif' data-full='https://slumber.lucaspickering.me/images/demo.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://horace.io/brrr_intro.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making deep learning go brrrr from first principles (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从第一性原理让深度学习跑起来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 04:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From first principles, this piece builds intuition for deep learning by focusing on core ideas like differentiable graphs, backpropagation, and optimization. It shows how simple concepts, engineered well, can power substantial performance gains without hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从第一性原理出发，聚焦可微分计算图、反向传播与优化等核心思想，帮助读者建立深度学习直觉。展示如何用简单原理通过仔细设计实现显著性能提升，而非靠炒作。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48225357' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/music/music-industry-news/spotify-will-start-reserving-concert-tickets-for-superfans-1236603106/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spotify will start reserving concert tickets for fans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Spotify 将为粉丝保留演唱会门票</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 09:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 357</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225357'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spotify will start reserving concert tickets for superfans, a feature that could improve fan loyalty and reduce scalping by giving verified fans early access. The move signals the platform’s shift from streaming-only to event monetization, potentially reshaping artist-ticket distribution and fan engagement. Expect more integrations between streaming profiles and live shows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Spotify 将为超级粉丝保留演唱会门票，这一举措有助于提升粉丝忠诚度并通过优先权减少黄牛。此举标志着平台从纯粁流媒体向活动票务与艺人生态的扩张，可能改变艺人票务分配与粉丝互动方式。未来可能看到流媒体资料与现场演出的更多整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2184690646-1.jpg?crop=0px%2C161px%2C4995px%2C2798px&amp;resize=1440%2C810' data-full='https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2184690646-1.jpg?crop=0px%2C161px%2C4995px%2C2798px&amp;resize=1440%2C810' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openculture.com/2026/05/archaeologists-discover-ancient-egyptian-mummy-buried-with-pages-from-homers-iliad.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Archaeologists find Egyptian mummy buried with the &#x27;Iliad&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>考古学家发现带有《伊利亚特》页的埃及木乃伊</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 15, 2026 / 15:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48154796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48154796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48154796'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists discovered a mummy with pages from the Iliad, illustrating cross-cultural exchange and the value placed on classical texts in antiquity. The find sheds light on funerary practices and the broader exchange of texts in antiquity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家在一具埃及木乃伊中发现《伊利亚特》的文本残页，揭示了古代社会中文本的跨文化传播与价值观。此发现为理解葬仪习俗及古代文本的流通提供新线索。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/amatsuda/rubish' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rubish: A Unix shell written in pure Ruby</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 23:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48245262'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rubish is a Unix shell implemented entirely in Ruby, illustrating how a high-level language can power a shell prototype and the trade-offs involved. It serves as a learning project for exploring shell design and Ruby metaprogramming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rubish 是一个完全用 Ruby 实现的 Unix shell，展示了用高级语言实现一个 shell 原型的可行性及其中的权衡。此项目适合作为探索 shell 设计与 Ruby 元编程的学习案例。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1024000^2 块、2B2T 世界下载计划及发现</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48180204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48180204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48180204'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project attempting to download the entire 2B2T world with a staggering 1.0e12 blocks reveals the scale of Minecraft&#x27;s procedurally generated terrain and the challenges of data capture. The effort yields insights into storage, chunking, and network transfer, along with surprising finds from the sprawling map. It underscores the tensions between archival ambition and practical limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次企图以约 1 万亿方块规模下载 2B2T 世界的工程暴露了 Minecraft 程序化生成地形的规模与数据捕获的挑战。该工作带来了存储、分块和传输方面的见解，并在庞大地图中发现了有趣的结构与遗迹。它凸显了档案化野心与现实极限之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 09:49 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48161772'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kv4p HT is a DIY handheld transceiver delivering about 1 watt, designed to plug into an Android phone. The project showcases a compact, open hardware approach to mobile amateur radio and raises considerations about licensing, shielding, and usability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kv4p HT 是一个自制的手持发射机，功率约为 1 瓦，设计为可直接连接 Android 手机使用。该项目体现了面向移动业余无线电的紧凑开放硬件思路，同时引发对许可证、屏蔽与易用性等问题的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/payphone-voip' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VoIP brings back old-fashioned pay phones to rural Vermont (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VoIP 技术在佛蒙特州农村重新部署电话亭（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 12:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48172505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48172505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48172505'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pilot uses VoIP-based payphones to fill gaps in rural Vermont where mobile coverage is spotty. Internet-connected kiosks offer emergency calling and everyday use, reviving a classic public amenity with modern networking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VoIP 技术在佛蒙特州农村重新部署电话亭，弥补移动覆盖薄弱带来的通信盲区。借助互联网连接，这些亭子既能提供紧急呼叫，也能完成日常通话，展示用现代网络改造旧有基础设施的尝试。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 15, 2026 / 16:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48155450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48155450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48155450'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists report crystals found inside wreckage from the Trinity test, offering new clues about the materials and processes involved in the early nuclear weapons experiment. The findings illuminate historical physics and materials science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在第一颗核弹试验（Trinity）残骸中发现晶体，提供关于早期核武器试验材料与工艺的新线索。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48235489' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvgze8n5dxko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alberta to hold referendum on whether to remain in Canada</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艾伯塔省将就是否留在加拿大举行公投</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 06:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48235489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48235489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 357</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48235489'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alberta plans a referendum on whether to remain in Canada, centering questions of provincial autonomy and resource governance. The outcome could influence federal-provincial dynamics and energy policy discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>艾伯塔省计划就是否留在加拿大举行公投，聚焦省级自治与资源治理等议题。结果可能影响联邦与省级关系以及能源政策的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/a48d/live/1799bfa0-5551-11f1-bdf3-a1cfc8f36fb3.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/a48d/live/1799bfa0-5551-11f1-bdf3-a1cfc8f36fb3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48221877' data-tags='Finance,Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48221877' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=6443103' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who wins and who loses in prediction markets? Evidence from Polymarket</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>预测市场中谁胜谁负？来自 Polymarket 的实证研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 05:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48221877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Evidence from Polymarket shows prediction markets can efficiently aggregate information, with winners typically better-informed traders and those who manage risk well; however, the market also faces liquidity and manipulation concerns. The research informs both the financial viability of prediction markets and regulatory considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自 Polymarket 的证据表明预测市场能够有效聚合信息，获利者通常是信息掌握更充分且风险管理能力更强的交易者；但市场也存在流动性不足和操纵等问题。这项研究为预测市场的经济可行性及监管考量提供了洞见。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48203536' data-tags='Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48203536' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.infomaniak.com/en/infomaniak-foundation-sovereign-cloud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Infomaniak transitions to a foundation model to protect user data privacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Infomaniak 转向基础模型以保护用户数据隐私</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 22:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48203536'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Infomaniak is adopting a foundation model approach to protect user data privacy. By housing the model stack in its sovereign cloud and tightening data handling controls, the company aims to minimize data exposure and improve user control over AI processing. The move highlights a growing trend of privacy-conscious providers considering foundation models as a way to balance AI usefulness with data governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Infomaniak 正在采用基础模型策略以提升数据隐私保护。通过将模型堆栈置于自有 souverign 云并强化数据处理控制，该公司旨在降低数据暴露风险并提高用户对 AI 处理的掌控感。这一举措反映出隐私导向的云服务商开始将基础模型用于在确保数据治理的同时发挥 AI 作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.infomaniak.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/infomaniak-foundation-banner.jpg' data-full='https://news.infomaniak.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/infomaniak-foundation-banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 10:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48171015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48171015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48171015'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU policymakers weigh restricting US cloud platforms for processing government data due to sovereignty and security concerns. The move could spur local cloud alternatives and reshape global cloud dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟在主权与安全担忧驱动下，权衡限制美国云平台处理政府数据。此举可能推动本地云方案的兴起并重塑全球云格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anomalyco/models.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Models.dev: open-source database of AI model specs, pricing, and capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Models.dev：开源的 AI 模型规格、定价与能力数据库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 13:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48241172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48241172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48241172'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Models.dev is an open-source database cataloging AI model specs, pricing, and capabilities, helping developers compare offerings and plan deployments. By aggregating model details, it lowers discovery friction and could influence how startups choose AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Models.dev 是一个开源数据库，汇总 AI 模型的规格、定价与能力，帮助开发者比较不同产品并制定部署计划。集中整理信息有助于降低发现成本，可能影响初创企业对 AI 服务的选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/dotnet/improving-csharp-memory-safety/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improving C# Memory Safety</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>提升 C# 内存安全性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 09:54 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225782'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft discusses strides in memory safety for C#, highlighting language features (nullable references, spans, or memory-safe patterns) and runtime improvements. The piece explains how these changes reduce common bugs while preserving performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软阐述了提升 C# 内存安全性的进展，聚焦可空引用、Span 等安全特性与运行时改进，说明这些变化如何降低常见错误并保持性能。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48259990' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48259990' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scienceaim.com/australia-just-proved-the-four-day-work-week-works-here-is-what-the-data-actually-says/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Australia Four-Day Work Week Study Data Shows Boosted Productivity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>澳大利亚四天工作周研究数据：生产力提升</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 11:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48259990'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Australian trials show that a four-day workweek can boost productivity without sacrificing output, while workers report lower stress and higher morale. The data adds to a growing body of evidence that shorter work weeks can be a viable option for knowledge workers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>澳大利亚的四天工作周试点数据显示，在压缩工作日的同时，生产力保持提升，员工压力和满意度也有所改善。数据进一步支持缩短工时对知识工作者的可行性及潜在政策影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scienceaim.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Screenshot_3.png' data-full='https://scienceaim.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Screenshot_3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48192605' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48192605' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brainbaking.com/post/2026/05/the-death-of-the-brick-and-mortar-toy-store/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The death of the brick and mortar toy store</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>实体玩具店的衰落</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 05:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48192605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48192605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48192605'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Brick-and-mortar toy stores are closing at growing rates as online shopping and digital entertainment siphon foot traffic and margins. The piece analyzes how inventory strategies, margins, and local economic factors are reshaping physical toy retail, with implications for creators and small businesses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实体玩具店因线上购物和数字娱乐分流客流与利润而迅速关闭。文章分析了存货策略、利润率和地方经济因素如何改变线下玩具零售格局，并对创作者与小企业的前景产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48179029' data-tags='AI,Business,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48179029' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/ai-ml/2026/05/17/enough-with-the-ai-fomo-go-slow-mo-says-domo-cdo/5240840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Enough with the AI FOMO, go slow-mo, says Domo CDO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Domo CDO：别再追逐 AI 的错失恐惧，要慢慢来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 05:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48179029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48179029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48179029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Domo&#x27;s Chief Data Officer urges a slower, more deliberate approach to AI adoption, warning against AI hype and stressing data quality, governance, and measurable ROI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Domo 首席数据官呼吁放慢对 AI 的追逐，抵制盲目炒作，强调数据质量、治理和可衡量投资回报的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/2940761.jpg?imageId=2940761&amp;x=0&amp;y=7.2&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=85.61&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=7.2&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=85.61&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/2940761.jpg?imageId=2940761&amp;x=0&amp;y=7.2&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=85.61&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=7.2&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=85.61&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48209558' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pvk.ca/Blog/2014/03/15/sbcl-the-ultimate-assembly-code-breadboard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SBCL: the ultimate assembly code breadboard (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SBCL：汇编代码的面包板（2014）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 08:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48209558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece positions SBCL as a practical playground for low-level experimentation inside a high-level Lisp system. It argues that Lisp macros and SBCL&#x27;s runtime make it easy to prototype and benchmark tiny assembly ideas without sacrificing safety. It&#x27;s a reminder of how language ecosystems can accelerate systems tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文把 SBCL 当作在高阶 Lisp 环境中进行底层实验的实用面包板，强调 Lisp 宏与 SBCL 运行时能在不牺牲安全性的前提下快速原型化和基准测试汇编思路。这也提醒读者，语言生态可以加速系统层面的试验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pvk.ca/images/2014-03-15-sbcl-the-ultimate-assembly-code-breadboard/rotating-stack.jpg' data-full='https://pvk.ca/images/2014-03-15-sbcl-the-ultimate-assembly-code-breadboard/rotating-stack.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48231547' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.firefox.com/en-US/landing/adafruit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Build Adafruit projects right from Firefox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 让 Adafruit 项目直接在浏览器构建</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 20:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48231547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox now enables building and interacting with Adafruit hardware projects directly from the browser, leveraging in-browser USB/Serial capabilities. This reduces setup friction for makers and demonstrates growing hardware-tethered experiences in the browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 现在支持直接在浏览器中构建并与 Adafruit 硬件项目互动，利用浏览器内的 USB/串口能力。为开发者和创客降低了上手难度，展现出浏览器在硬件协作方面日益成熟的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.firefox.com/media/img/firefox/flare/og.4d50d9b1674b.png' data-full='https://www.firefox.com/media/img/firefox/flare/og.4d50d9b1674b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48183097' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/what-do-godels-incompleteness-theorems-truly-mean-20260518/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Do Gödel&#x27;s Incompleteness Theorems Mean?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>哥德尔不完备性定理到底意味着什么？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 10:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gödel&#x27;s incompleteness theorems are explained in accessible terms, clarifying what they mean for formal systems and the limits of proving mathematical truths within a fixed set of rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对哥德尔不完备性定理进行通俗解释，阐明它们对形式系统的局限以及在固定公理体系内证明数学真理的边界。文中讨论了对计算机科学、人工智能与证明本质的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Qualia-Incompleteness-cr-Kurt-Godel-Papers-the-Shelby-White-and-Leon-Levy-Achives-Center-Institute-for-Advanced-Study-Samuel-Velasco-and-Michael-Kanyongolo-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Qualia-Incompleteness-cr-Kurt-Godel-Papers-the-Shelby-White-and-Leon-Levy-Achives-Center-Institute-for-Advanced-Study-Samuel-Velasco-and-Michael-Kanyongolo-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ox.ac.uk/news/2026-05-15-why-is-almost-everyone-right-handed-the-answer-may-lie-in-how-we-learned-to-walk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is almost everyone right-handed? A new study connects it to bipedalism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>几乎所有人都用右手？新研究把这一现象与直立行走的演化联系起来</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 07:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48194098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48194098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48194098'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study links right-handedness to the evolution of bipedalism, suggesting brain lateralization and motor control co-evolved with walking on two legs. The finding adds a biological lens to everyday human behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项新研究将右利手现象与直立行走的演化联系起来，暗示大脑半球化与运动控制在双足行走中共同演化。该发现为日常行为提供了新的生物学视角。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ShadowCat：浏览器内通过二维码传输文件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 04:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ShadowCat enables file transfers via QR codes inside a browser, offering a lightweight, privacy-preserving transfer method without server dependencies. This approach showcases how browser-first tools can enable quick P2P sharing with low setup friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ShadowCat 在浏览器中通过二维码实现文件传输，提供一种轻量且注重隐私的传输方式，无需服务器依赖。此类浏览器优先的工具展示了在低门槛下实现点对点共享的可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author chronicles ongoing friction with Scheme, a minimalist Lisp dialect, and reflects on its clean semantics versus a steep learning curve. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者记录了自己在学习 Scheme 时不断遇到的困难， Scheme 的极简 Lisp 语法与清晰语义与陡峭的学习曲线形成对比。文章给出在实际项目中何时因其简洁而受益、何时又因学习成本而成为拖累的洞察。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cato.org/blog/dhs-quits-granting-green-cards-almost-entirely' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DHS Quits Granting Green Cards–Almost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>国土安全部几乎停止发放绿卡</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 04:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48246735'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DHS has effectively halted new green card grants, signaling a shift in immigration processing and potential backlogs for applicants and employers. The move raises concerns about workforce planning, family reunification timelines, and policy enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国土安全部已实质性地暂停新绿卡发放，标志着移民处理流程的重大转变及申请人与雇主可能面临的积压。此举引发对人力规划、家庭团聚的等待期以及政策执行的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 12:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48260140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48260140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48260140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CBP&#x27;s directive clarifies border search authority for electronic devices, outlining procedures, privacy considerations, and data handling. The guidance informs travelers and developers about what can be inspected and how data is stored or used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CBP 的指令明确了边境对电子设备搜查的权限与程序，涵盖隐私考量与数据处理。为旅行者和开发者提供了对可检查内容以及数据存储与使用方式的指引。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48257980' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48257980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/may/24/ai-washing-pr-firms-scrambling-rebrand' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;AI washing&#x27;: firms are scrambling to rebrand themselves as tech-focused</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 洗白：企业争相包装成以技术为核心的公司</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 08:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48257980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48257980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48257980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many firms are rushing to rebrand as technology-focused AI players, often without meaningful AI capabilities, a trend critics call AI washing. The piece surveys examples and explains why hype can outpace real product and talent investments, risking credibility and investor trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>许多公司急于把自己包装成以 AI 为核心的科技企业，往往缺乏实质性的 AI 能力，这种趋势被称为 AI 洗白。文中梳理了若干案例，解释了炒作如何赶超实际产品与人才投入，侵蚀可信度和投资者信任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6c4dd4b2880760003ffbd14e8d6884b367237ad3/1844_305_2349_1879/master/2349.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8ae6c8a540881dcdf228bf87820a2733' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/6c4dd4b2880760003ffbd14e8d6884b367237ad3/1844_305_2349_1879/master/2349.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8ae6c8a540881dcdf228bf87820a2733' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48183167' data-tags='Career'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48246917' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48246917' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/YoavCodes/status/2058064720553222567' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electrobun 2.0 will be decoupled from Bun due to the Rust rewrite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Electrobun 2.0 将因重写为 Rust 而与 Bun 解耦</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 04:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48246917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48246917'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Electrobun 2.0 is being decoupled from Bun as a Rust rewrite advances, signaling modular architecture and performance goals. The shift matters for developers depending on Bun for fast tooling and for understanding how language runtimes evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于用 Rust 重写，Electrobun 2.0 将与 Bun 分离，朝向更模块化、性能导向的架构。此变动对依赖 Bun 的开发者及运行时演进具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1920854672216064000/-iUcVNDF_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1920854672216064000/-iUcVNDF_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48227923' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48227923' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Multi-Stream LLMs: new paper on parallelizing/separating prompts, thinking, I/O</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多流大模型：关于并行化/分离提示、思考与 I/O 的新论文</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 12:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48227923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48227923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48227923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new paper on Multi-Stream LLMs proposes parallelizing prompts, internal thinking, and I/O to improve throughput and reasoning. The idea is to run multiple streams of computation in tandem to reduce latency and better manage resources, with potential implications for scalable AI systems and tool-using agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇关于多流大模型的新论文提出将提示、推理过程和输入输出并行化，以提升吞吐量与推理效率。通过并行计算流来降低延迟、优化资源管理，对可扩展 AI 系统与具工具代理的应用具有潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48256475' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mastering.dyalog.com/README.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mastering Dyalog APL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>精通 Dyalog APL</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 04:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48256475'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dyalog APL remains one of the most expressive array languages; this resource pack promises practical guidance—from idioms to performance tricks—to help you write concise, fast APL code. It emphasizes vectorized thinking and how to leverage Dyalog’s extensions for real-world data tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dyalog APL 是最具表达力的数组语言之一，这份资源集合提供实用指南—from 常用习惯用法到性能技巧，帮助你写出简洁且高效的 APL 代码。文章强调向量化思维，以及如何利用 Dyalog 的扩展应对实际数据任务。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48182872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A longform piece uses Hanoi&#x27;s humble beer glass as a lens to explore Vietnamese memory and national identity, showing how everyday objects can embody collective history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇长文以河内朴素的啤酒杯为线索，探讨越南的记忆与国家认同。日常物件如何成为集体历史的载体以及在社会记忆中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48254020' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-alexander-grothendieck-revolutionized-20th-century-mathematics-20260520/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alexander Grothendieck Revolutionized 20th-Century Mathematics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚历山大·格罗滕迪克如何革新了20世纪数学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 20:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48254020'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grothendieck profoundly reshaped 20th-century mathematics by advancing categories, toposes, and structural thinking. The piece traces how his ideas redirected the field toward abstraction and unifying frameworks that influenced algebraic geometry and beyond.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格罗滕迪克通过推进范畴、层概念以及结构化思维，深刻改变了20世纪的数学。文章梳理了他如何把数学推向高度抽象的统一框架，深远影响了代数几何及其他领域。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Grothendieck-cr-Mercedes-deBellard-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Grothendieck-cr-Mercedes-deBellard-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48209905' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48209905' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pacifichorticulture.org/articles/mycorrhizal-fungi-natures-key-to-plant-survival-and-success/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mycorrhizal Fungi, Nature&#x27;s Key to Plant Survival and Success</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>菌根真菌：植物生存与繁盛的自然之钥</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 09:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209905' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48209905'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mycorrhizal fungi form networks that extend plant root systems, aiding nutrient and water uptake and enabling communication between plants. Understanding this symbiosis has practical implications for sustainable agriculture and ecosystem resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>菌根真菌通过形成网络扩展植物根系，帮助营养和水分吸收，并促进同群植物之间的信号传递。理解这一共生关系对可持续农业和生态系统韧性具有现实意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48162876' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48162876' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fil-c.org/calling_convention' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Fil-C Optimized Calling Convention</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fil-C 的优化调用约定</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 12:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48162876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48162876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48162876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Fil-C calling convention outlines practical optimizations for function calls, including argument-passing rules, register usage, and ABI stability. The spec aims to reduce call overhead and boost performance for Fil-C tooling and applications in its ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fil-C 调用约定概述了函数调用的实际优化，包括参数传递规则、寄存器使用与 ABI 稳定性。该规范旨在降低调用开销、提升 Fil-C 工具链和应用的性能，并促进生态内的互操作性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48232721'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A neat TTRPG utility that uses a thermal printer to print game content on demand, lowering setup costs for tabletop sessions. The project demonstrates approachable hardware-software integration and a playful take on RPG tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款有趣的桌面角色扮演游戏（TTRPG）工具，利用热敏打印机按需输出游戏内容，降低了设置成本。该项目展示了软硬件集成的可操作性与对 RPG 场景的创新应用。</p>
