<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48209055' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48209055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/wi-wi-is-wireless-time-sync-less-than-5ns/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wi-Wi is wireless time sync at 1 nanosecond</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wi-Wi：无线时间同步约1纳秒</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 08:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48209055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wi-Wi demonstrates wireless time synchronization with sub-nanosecond precision, offering a cable-free path to synchronizing distributed systems. If broadly deployable, it could simplify clock alignment for data centers, edge networks, and IoT, though practical adoption hinges on hardware and interference considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wi-Wi 展示了无线时间同步的亚纳秒级精度，为分布式系统提供无缆对时方案。若广泛应用，可能简化数据中心、边缘网络与物联网的时钟对齐，但实际落地取决于硬件支持与干扰等因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/wi-wi-is-wireless-time-sync-less-than-5ns/wi-wi-stamp-leader-timing-hardware.jpg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/wi-wi-is-wireless-time-sync-less-than-5ns/wi-wi-stamp-leader-timing-hardware.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48174808' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48174808' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/644853/pew-gallup-data-americans-dont-trust-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most Americans don&#x27;t trust AI – or the people in charge of it (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数美国人不信任 AI 及掌控者（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 18:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48174808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48174808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48174808'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pew/Gallup data show broad American distrust of AI and the people shaping it in 2025. The sentiment spans technology, governance, and corporate motives, posing legitimacy challenges for AI initiatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>皮尤/盖洛普数据表明，2025 年多数美国人对 AI 及其掌控者缺乏信任，涉及科技、治理与企业动机，给 AI 项目带来公信力挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/04/STK_414_AI_CHATBOT_R2_CVirginia_A.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/04/STK_414_AI_CHATBOT_R2_CVirginia_A.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A faithful recreation of the Commodore 64&#x27;s iconic Dead Test font, enabling pixel-perfect retro typography in modern projects. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>忠实再现了 Commodore 64 的 Dead Test 位图字形，使现代项目能够实现像素级的复古字体效果。此字体便于在仿真器、演示和教学材料中再现 C64 的风格，而无需依赖旧 ROM。显示出在演示与教育场景中对复古排版真实性的持续需求。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48178378' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48178378' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/voice-ai-audio-attacks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Voice AI Systems Are Vulnerable to Hidden Audio Attacks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>语音 AI 系统易受隐藏音频攻击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 04:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48178378'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show that voice AI systems can be manipulated by hidden audio attacks—commands played at inaudible frequencies. The vulnerability matters for consumer devices and enterprise systems alike, highlighting a need for robust microphone filtering, anomaly detection, and adversarial audio defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员表明，语音 AI 系统可能被听不见的隐藏音频攻击所利用，发出不可听见的指令即可执行。此漏洞对消费级设备和企业系统都具有现实意义，强调需要加强麦克风滤波、异常检测以及对抗性音频防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66745304&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C114%2C0%2C136' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=66745304&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C114%2C0%2C136' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成长型神经元细胞自动机</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48172320'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Growing Neural Cellular Automata extends neural networks to control the growth of cellular automata, enabling shapes to emerge from a single cell and even exhibit self-healing. The work demonstrates robust, emergent design principles with potential implications for biology-inspired computation and scalable hardware-like systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>成长型神经元细胞自动机让神经网络控制细胞自动机的生长，能从单个细胞生成形状，甚至具备自我修复能力。该工作展示了可自发演化的鲁棒设计原理，对生物启发计算和可扩展的硬件式系统具有潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://susam.net/do-not-roll-your-own.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t Roll Your Own</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要自己造轮子：不要自己实现加密算法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 15:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48252192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48252192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48252192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Don&#x27;t Roll Your Own argues against implementing cryptography from scratch; instead, rely on vetted libraries and standards to avoid subtle flaws and security risks. It provides guidelines for when to use established crypto primitives and how to securely compose them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不要自己造轮子：不要从零实现加密算法；应使用经过验证的库和标准，以避免细微缺陷和安全风险。文中给出在何时应选用成熟的密码学原语以及如何安全地组合它们的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ikesau.co/blog/defeating-git-rigour-fatigue-with-jujutsu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Defeating Git Rigour Fatigue with Jujutsu</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以柔术化解 Git 的严格流程疲劳</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 11:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48259861'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pragmatic take argues against rigid Git workflows that burn out teams, offering lightweight, jujutsu-inspired practices to reduce cognitive load. Expect tips like smaller pull requests, incremental changes, and flexible branching to maintain momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为对 Git 的过度严格流程会让团队疲惫，主张用“柔术式”的简化方法降低认知负荷。给出可操作的建议，如缩短提交范围、分支策略更灵活、逐步变更以保持开发势头。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nodejs.org/en/blog/release/v26.0.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Node.js 26.0.0 (Now with Temporal)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Node.js 26.0.0：现内置 Temporal</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 11:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48212242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48212242'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Node.js 26.0.0 adds Temporal, a modern date/time API, to the runtime, promising safer handling of calendars and timestamps. The release touches on developer ergonomics and potential shifts in how time is modeled in JavaScript apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Node.js 26.0.0 将 Temporal 引入运行时，提供更现代的日期时间 API，提升日历和时间戳处理的安全性。此举对开发者的体验与 JavaScript 应用中的时间建模产生潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nodejs.org/en/next-data/og/release/Node.js%20%E2%80%94%20Node.js%2026.0.0%20(Current)' data-full='https://nodejs.org/en/next-data/og/release/Node.js%20%E2%80%94%20Node.js%2026.0.0%20(Current)' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48204985' data-tags='Science,Design,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48204985' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://horses.extension.org/blood-pumping-mechanism-of-the-hoof/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blood Pumping Mechanism of the Hoof (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蹄部血液泵送机制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 02:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48204985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48204985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48204985'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Research into the hoof shows a unique mechanism that helps pump blood back toward the body during locomotion, relying on tendons and the hoof&#x27;s geometry. Understanding this helps veterinarians and breeders optimize hoof health and manage circulation-related issues in horses. It also informs bioinspired design of soft robotics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对马蹄的研究揭示了一种独特的血液回流机制，依靠肌腱和蹄部几何形状在运动中推动血液回至身体。这些发现有助于兽医与繁育者优化蹄部健康、管理循环相关问题，并为软体机器人等生物启发设计提供灵感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://horses.extension.org/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/EH-2.png' data-full='https://horses.extension.org/wp-content/uploads/2020/01/EH-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48177300' data-tags='Security,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48177300' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.csl.sri.com/users/neumann/psos.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The foundations of a provably secure operating system (PSOS) (1979) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可证明安全操作系统（PSOS）的奠基</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 02:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48177300'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1979 paper outlines a framework for an operating system whose security properties can be proven mathematically, addressing access control, isolation, and trusted computing base. It foreshadows modern formal methods and formal verification approaches used in systems like seL4 and other provably secure designs, highlighting enduring challenges in proving security properties in real-world OS design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1979年的这篇基础性论文提出一种可通过形式化推理来证明安全性的操作系统设计框架，聚焦访问控制、隔离性与受信计算基。它预示了现代形式化方法和操作系统安全证明的方向，并揭示在现实系统中实现可证明安全性所面临的难题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联网门铃若配置不当，可能被任意人远程触发，带来隐私与安全隐患。文中指出暴露面、认证薄弱，以及改进补丁和访问控制的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48194857' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1002/spe.70079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Converting an Integer to a Decimal String in Under Two Nanoseconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在不到两纳秒内将整数转换为十进制字符串</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48194857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48194857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48194857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes an approach to render integers as decimal strings in under two nanoseconds on modern hardware, using a combination of branchless arithmetic and precomputed tables. The result highlights the edge of micro-optimizations in serialization, with implications for high-throughput logging and performance-critical systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在现代硬件上将整数转换为十进制字符串的极限方法，时间不到两纳秒，结合分支极小化的运算与预计算表。该成果凸显序列化微优化的边界，对高吞吐日志和性能敏感的系统具有启示意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>z386 is an open-source recreation of the 80386 CPU built around authentic microcode, offering an educational glimpse into early x86 internals and a bridge between vintage hardware and modern tooling. The project tackles challenges of emulating legacy microcode and provides insight for hardware tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源重现的80386 CPU 项目 z386 基于原始微代码，提供了学习早期 x86 内部结构的机会。该项目在复现旧微代码方面面临挑战，并为硬件爱好者提供实践案例。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48210937' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ClickHouse/silk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Silk: Open-source cooperative fiber scheduler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Silk：开源的协作式纤程调度器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 10:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48210937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48210937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48210937'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Silk is an open-source cooperative scheduler designed to efficiently manage many lightweight tasks by yielding control explicitly. It enables high throughput with low overhead, fitting into data-processing stacks like ClickHouse. It’s a notable alternative to preemptive kernels for certain workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Silk 是一个开源的协作式调度器，通过显式让出控制来高效管理大量轻量任务。它在低开销下实现高吞吐，适用于数据处理堆栈（如 ClickHouse），是对特定工作负载的有力替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/be72d61958ffbd3326bc77aeb1cd87b5d4ac09465852c4d39b148d09d7553cbc/ClickHouse/silk' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/be72d61958ffbd3326bc77aeb1cd87b5d4ac09465852c4d39b148d09d7553cbc/ClickHouse/silk' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://odyssey.ml/introducing-agora-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agora-1: The Multi-Agent World Model</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 11:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183748'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agora-1 introduces a world model tailored for multi-agent environments, enabling agents to reason, coordinate, and plan within a shared model of the world. This work advances scalable, end-to-end reasoning for complex multi-agent tasks and aids research into emergent collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agora-1 面向多智能体场景推出了一个世界模型，使智能体能够在共用的世界表示中进行推理、协调和规划。该工作推动对复杂多智能体任务的端到端推理能力，并有助于研究协作与涌现行为。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48190373' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jvns.ca/blog/2023/12/04/mounting-git-commits-as-folders-with-nfs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mounting git commits as folders with NFS (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 NFS 将 Git 提交挂载为文件夹</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 00:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48190373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48190373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48190373'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mounting Git commits as folders via NFS lets you browse repository history as if each commit were a distinct directory. It simplifies quick visual inspection of tree states across machines but may incur performance and metadata pitfalls. Useful for exploration, less for production workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 NFS 将 Git 提交挂载为文件夹，使你可以把每个提交当作独立目录来浏览提交树的状态。便于跨设备快速查看历史分支，但可能带来性能和元数据方面的问题，适合探索用途而非正式生产工作流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 14:41 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48214558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX&#x27;s Starship undergoes its 12th flight test, pushing the vehicle through a rigorous, iterative test program. Each test sharpens risk assessment and schedule for future flights.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://security.apple.com/blog/formal-verification-corecrypto/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A blueprint for formal verification of Apple corecrypto</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48239862'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple outlines a blueprint for formally verifying CoreCrypto, signaling a shift toward mathematically proven security for critical cryptographic code. The plan could influence how cryptographic libraries are developed and audited across the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果提出对 CoreCrypto 进行形式化验证的蓝图，标志着对关键密码实现进行严格数学证明的安全方向。此举可能影响业界在加密库开发与审核方面的做法。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>感知图像编解码：实用学习型图像压缩中的要点</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 05:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48256565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48256565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This overview distills what actually matters in practical learned image compression: perceptual quality, rate-distortion trade-offs, training data, and deployment robustness. It argues for metrics and architectures that maintain perceptual fidelity under real-world constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提炼了实用的学习型图像压缩的关键要点：感知质量、码率-失真权衡、训练数据与落地稳健性。作者主张在实际约束下，选择能维持感知保真度的指标与架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://apple.github.io/ml-pico/assets/arxiv-icon.svg' data-full='https://apple.github.io/ml-pico/assets/arxiv-icon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48168159' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48168159' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260514-how-hallucinogenic-ibogaine-helps-veterans-overcome-ptsd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trials on veterans suggest ibogaine could provide a new treatment for PTSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>退伍军人研究表明 ibogaine 可能为 PTSD 提供新治疗方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 05:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48168159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48168159'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early trials suggest ibogaine could offer a new treatment for PTSD in veterans, possibly via neuroplasticity mechanisms. More research is needed to establish safety, dosing, and long-term outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在退伍军人中的早期试验表明 ibogaine 可能为 PTSD 提供新疗法，或通过促进神经可塑性实现缓解。仍需进一步研究以确定安全性、剂量和长期效果。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yes-we-scan.app/details' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reviving old scanners with an in-browser Linux VM bridged to WebUSB over USB/IP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过浏览器内的 Linux 虚拟机、并通过 WebUSB/USB/IP 桥接，重启了旧扫描仪并实现对硬件的无驱动访问</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 03:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48177467'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project resurrects old scanners by running a Linux VM in the browser, bridged via WebUSB and USB/IP, enabling hardware access without native drivers. It showcases browser-powered hardware experimentation and retro-fit ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目通过在浏览器中运行 Linux 虚拟机、并通过 WebUSB/USB/IP 桥接，重启了旧扫描仪并实现对硬件的无驱动访问。此举展示了浏览器驱动的硬件实验与复古兼容的思路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jdhodges.com/blog/thinkpad-history/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The History of ThinkPad: From IBM’s Bento Box to Lenovo’s AI Workstations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ThinkPad 的历史：从 IBM 的 Bento Box 到 Lenovo 的 AI 工作站</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 14:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48173547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48173547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective traces ThinkPad&#x27;s journey from IBM&#x27;s era and its iconic &#x27;Bento Box&#x27; design to Lenovo&#x27;s modern shift toward AI-accelerated workstations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾 ThinkPad 的发展史：从 IBM 时代的标志性设计到联想在 AI 时代的工作站定位的转变。通过设计与定位的变迁，揭示了企业移动性与硬件设计文化的演进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48236770' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/superset-sh/superset' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Superset (YC P26) – IDE for the agents era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Superset（YC P26）——面向代理时代的 IDE</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 07:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48236770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48236770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48236770'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Superset positions itself as an IDE for the agents era, focusing on composing, debugging, and orchestrating AI agents and tool use. It reflects a broader shift toward automation-first developer workflows and tools that help manage multi-agent collaboration across tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Superset 定位为“代理时代”的集成开发环境，聚焦编排、调试与协同AI代理及工具的使用。反映了向自动化优先的开发工作流转变，以及帮助管理多代理在不同任务中的协作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4aee1b340fc1ca2e7b9116a7f5ded3ea3f32e484622c4eb0b1414ff5d9d2da17/superset-sh/superset' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4aee1b340fc1ca2e7b9116a7f5ded3ea3f32e484622c4eb0b1414ff5d9d2da17/superset-sh/superset' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48232118' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48232118' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CODA: Rewriting Transformer Blocks as GEMM-Epilogue Programs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CODA：将 Transformer 块改写为 GEMM-Epilogue 程序</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 21:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48232118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48232118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48232118'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CODA presents a method to map transformer blocks into sequences of GEMM-epilogue programs, enabling closer use of optimized GEMM backends and better memory locality. This approach aims to boost throughput on modern accelerators by restructuring core neural-net operations without changing model topology. It highlights how rethinking operators can unlock efficiency gains at the compiler and hardware interface.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CODA 提出一种将 Transformer 的计算映射为 GEMM-Epilogue 序列的方法，从而更充分地利用优化的 GEMM 后端并提升内存局部性。这一思路通过重构核心神经网络运算，以在不改变模型结构的前提下提升在现代加速器上的吞吐量，体现了在编译器与硬件接口层重新设计运算的潜在收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48209691' data-tags='Space,Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48209691' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/culture/article/20260518-helen-sharman-the-story-behind-the-first-british-person-in-space' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The first British person in space</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国首位太空人背后的惊人故事</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 08:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48209691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48209691'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Helen Sharman became the UK&#x27;s first astronaut after a televised competition and a Cold War-era collaboration with the Soviet space program. Her unlikely path, driven by public science education and serendipity, underscored how non-traditional routes can open access to space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>赫伦·夏尔曼通过一场公开的电视竞赛和 Cold War 时期的苏联太空计划合作成为英国首位太空人。她的非同寻常之路依赖公众科普与偶然机遇，凸显了非传统路径也能打开通往太空的大门。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nlxq9k.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nlxq9k.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48176762' data-tags='Web,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48176762' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reddit.com/r/pasadena/s/94BHlkE84r' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is Google Maps back to showing old satellite images of Altadena?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌地图为何重新显示 Altadena 的旧卫星影像</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 01:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48176762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48176762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48176762'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Observers noted Google Maps reverting to older satellite imagery for Altadena, prompting questions about data sources, licensing, and update cadence. The article explains how imagery updates can lag or temporarily revert due to data sourcing decisions, storage considerations, or rights constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观察者发现谷歌地图在 Altadena 显示的是旧卫星影像，引发对数据源、许可与更新节奏的关注。报道指出卫星影像更新可能因数据来源、存取许可或存储成本等原因出现滞后或短暂回退。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48217052' data-tags='AI,Finance,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48217052' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/05/20/openai-ipo-filing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI to confidentially file for IPO as soon as Friday</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 计划最早本周五秘密提交IPO申请</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 19:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48217052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48217052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48217052'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI plans a confidential IPO filing as early as Friday, a high-profile move into public markets that would invite scrutiny of its revenue model and long-term profitability. The move could reshape expectations around AI lab funding and growth paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 计划最早本周五秘密提交IPO申请，这将标志其在公开市场的高曝光度，并引发对其收入模式与长期盈利能力的关注。此举可能重塑人们对AI实验室融资与增长路径的预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108276787-1773253292188-gettyimages-2265445220-BLACKROCK_INFRASTRUCTURE_SUMMIT.jpeg?v=1779296174&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108276787-1773253292188-gettyimages-2265445220-BLACKROCK_INFRASTRUCTURE_SUMMIT.jpeg?v=1779296174&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at using Lisp in web apps around 2001, arguing Lisp’s expressiveness and macro system could outperform traditional web stacks in some scenarios, while practical barriers limited adoption. Provides historical context for language debates in early web development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾性论文回顾了 2001 年左右在网页应用中使用 Lisp 的情况，认为 Lisp 的表达力和宏系统在某些场景下可能优于传统网页栈，但现实障碍限制了广泛采用，为早期语言选择的讨论提供了历史视角。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 04:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chess invariants examine stable properties of positions across games, offering insights into endgames and strategic reasoning that remain constant under moves. These invariants can inform puzzle design and AI evaluation of position quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>棋局不变量聚焦于跨局对局中保持不变的稳定性质，为残局与策略推理提供稳定基线。这些不变量可应用于棋局谜题设计与AI对局评估中的位置质量判断。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.runtm.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Runtime (YC P26) – Sandboxed coding agents for everyone on a team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN: Runtime（YC P26）——为团队中的每个人提供沙盒化编码代理</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 09:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48225040' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48225040'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Launch HN introduces Runtime, a YC P26 company building sandboxed coding agents for teams. The idea is to let safe, restricted AI agents run in isolated sandboxes to assist coding without risking the broader system. Early-stage, promising for improving team collaboration if governance and security are well-handled.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Launch HN 推出 Runtime，YC 第26轮创业公司，面向团队提供沙盒化编码代理。该思路让经受严格隔离的 AI 助手在沙盒中协助编程，降低对整体系统的风险。处于初期阶段，若治理与安全得当，或提升团队协作效率。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fatgid.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FatGid: FreeBSD 14.x kernel local privilege escalation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FatGid：FreeBSD 14.x 内核本地权限提升漏洞披露</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 05:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48221415'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FatGid identifies a local privilege escalation in the FreeBSD 14.x kernel. The disclosure details a vulnerability that could allow unprivileged users to gain elevated privileges on affected systems, with advisories and patches likely on the horizon. It underscores ongoing kernel security research in BSD ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FatGid 指出 FreeBSD 14.x 内核中的本地权限提升漏洞。漏洞可能让未授权用户获得提升权限，相关公告和修补程序预计将随之发布，凸显了 BSD 生态系统中持续的内核安全研究。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48206763' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48206763' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2025-09-science-spaghetti-neutron-gluten-free.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neutron scattering explains why gluten-free pasta falls apart (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中子散射解释无麸质意面易碎的原因（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 05:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48206763'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neutron scattering revealed how water, starch, and protein interactions destabilize gluten-free pasta under heat and moisture. The findings guide better formulation and processing for gluten-free products, improving texture and cooking resilience. It contributes to broader insights into food science and material properties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过中子散射研究，科学家揭示了在加热和潮湿条件下，水分、淀粉与蛋白质相互作用如何导致无麸质意面易断裂。研究结果为无麸质产品的配方与加工提供改进方向，提升口感与耐煮性，并为食品科学与材料性能领域提供更广泛的洞见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2025/the-science-of-spaghet.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2025/the-science-of-spaghet.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48177716' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48177716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spacedaily.com/nasa-still-maintains-some-of-the-voyager-spacecraft-code-in-a-1970s-era-programming-language-that-almost-nobody-on-earth-fully-understands-anymore-and-the-handful-of-engineers-who-do-are-now-in-their/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NASA still maintains some of the Voyager spacecraft code from the 70s era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NASA 仍在维护 70 年代的 Voyager 航天器代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 03:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48177716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA still maintains Voyager spacecraft code from the 70s, kept in a language few engineers fully understand, reflecting long-term knowledge preservation and aging staff.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 仍在维护 70 年代的 Voyager 航天器的软件代码，使用极少数工程师能理解的语言，体现长期知识保留与人员老龄化挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spacedaily.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/newimagesize-2026-05-16T111530.760.png' data-full='https://spacedaily.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/newimagesize-2026-05-16T111530.760.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48165283' data-tags='Culture,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48165283' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thehistoryoftheweb.com/the-lasting-influence-of-netscape-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The lasting influence of Netscape Time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Netscape Time 的持久影响</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 18:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48165283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48165283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48165283'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of how Netscape&#x27;s early web era set lasting molds for the Internet economy—browser wars, rapid feature cycles, and open web ethos. The piece argues that the Netscape moment seeded practices around product iteration, open standards, and venture-backed scale that still shape tech firms today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾早期网景时期对互联网经济的长期影响——浏览器之争、快速迭代、开放网络精神等。作者认为这段历史奠定了产品迭代、开放标准和风险资本驱动扩张等做法，至今影响着科技公司的运作方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ik.imagekit.io/aoi3fgebjgr/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/netscape-navigator-1-0-01.png' data-full='https://ik.imagekit.io/aoi3fgebjgr/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/netscape-navigator-1-0-01.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48206313' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48206313' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bumbershootsoft.wordpress.com/2026/05/09/comparing-an-lz4-decompressor-on-four-legacy-cpus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Comparing an LZ4 Decompressor on Four Legacy CPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在四款遗留 CPU 上比较 LZ4 解压缩性能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 04:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48206313'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Benchmark results compare an LZ4 decompressor across four legacy CPUs, highlighting how processor age influences decompression throughput and efficiency. The findings inform optimization choices for embedded systems and legacy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基准测试比较了四款遗留 CPU 上的 LZ4 解压缩性能，揭示处理器老化对解压吞吐率的影响。结果为嵌入式系统与遗留工作负载的优化提供参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48239347' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48239347' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/sports-and-active-living/articles/10.3389/fspor.2026.1807791/full' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A scoping review of bicycling interventions’ impacts on well-being</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自行车干预对身心福祉影响的范围性综述</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 11:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48239347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48239347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48239347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A scoping review aggregates evidence on how cycling interventions affect well-being. Overall findings suggest improvements in physical health and mood, but results vary by intensity, duration, and population, highlighting gaps in long-term outcomes and equity considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>综述汇总了自行车干预对身心福祉的影响。总体显示对体质和情绪有积极作用，但效果因强度、时长和人群而异，长期效果与公平性研究仍存在空白。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/6D634989-85A0-4B57-8F10218E64DCB833/AA676D9F-0F67-4506-BCD1F92A3E4BA65C/WebsiteWebP_XL-FSPOR_Main Visual_Red_Website.webp' data-full='https://d2csxpduxe849s.cloudfront.net/media/E32629C6-9347-4F84-81FEAEF7BFA342B3/6D634989-85A0-4B57-8F10218E64DCB833/AA676D9F-0F67-4506-BCD1F92A3E4BA65C/WebsiteWebP_XL-FSPOR_Main Visual_Red_Website.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48206543' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48206543' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/new-high-resolution-map-transforms-what-we-know-about-roman-roads-and-the-roman-empire/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New map reveals lost roads of the Roman Empire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新地图揭示罗马帝国失落的道路</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 05:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48206543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48206543'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new high-resolution map reconstructs ancient Roman road networks, revealing previously unknown routes and &#x27;lost&#x27; paths. The map reshapes understanding of imperial logistics, trade corridors, and settlement patterns, and showcases how modern GIS and archaeology converge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张高分辨率的新地图重新绘制了古罗马公路网络，揭示了此前未知的路线与“失落的道路”。新地图改变了对帝国物流、贸易走廊与城镇布局的认识，展示了现代 GIS 与考古学的协同潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/bcce9619-01d3-4048-83c9-41a63d7554bb/sa0626Brug01.jpg?m=1778851821.765&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/bcce9619-01d3-4048-83c9-41a63d7554bb/sa0626Brug01.jpg?m=1778851821.765&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48176384' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48176384' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Mercury-Language/mercury' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Mercury logic programming system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mercury 逻辑编程系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 00:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48176384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48176384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48176384'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercury is a logic programming language and environment emphasizing reliability through a strong type and mode system. It offers a modern take on logic programming with tooling geared toward both research and education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 是一门强调通过强类型与模式系统提升可靠性的逻辑编程语言及环境。它以现代化工具链为特征，适用于研究与教学场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b76ea31da04ad20e52906fce18d100e9e79c1a546d248b86365b384f008c5304/Mercury-Language/mercury' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b76ea31da04ad20e52906fce18d100e9e79c1a546d248b86365b384f008c5304/Mercury-Language/mercury' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48235033' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcguide.com/news/valve-removes-free-horror-game-from-steam-after-players-discover-it-contains-malware-that-steals-your-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valve removes free game from Steam after players discover it contains malware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valve 在玩家发现免费游戏含有恶意软件后从 Steam 下架该作</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 05:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48235033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48235033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48235033'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve removes a free horror game from Steam after players discover malware that steals data, illustrating ongoing risk in storefront curation and the importance of proactive malware monitoring. The move underscores the need for rapid response to protect users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在玩家发现免费恐怖游戏包含会窃取数据的恶意软件后，Valve 从 Steam 下架该作。这凸显了商店验收与恶意软件监测的持续风险，以及对用户保护的快速响应的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pcguide.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Beyond-The-Dark-gameplay-screenshot-brightened.jpg' data-full='https://www.pcguide.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Beyond-The-Dark-gameplay-screenshot-brightened.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48183031' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/05/17/middleeast/iran-hormuz-undersea-cables-intl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran will impose fees on subsea internet cables in Strait of Hormuz</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗将就霍尔木兹海峡的海底光缆征收费用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 10:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183031'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iran plans to levy fees on subsea internet cables transiting the Strait of Hormuz, signaling efforts to monetize critical infrastructure amid regional tensions. The move could affect international bandwidth costs and data routing for multiple regions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗计划对经霍尔木兹海峡的海底光缆征收费用，显示其在紧张局势中对关键基础设施进行货币化的努力。此举可能影响多地区的国际带宽成本与数据路由。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/19-2026-05-01t103455z-921331245-rc210la8oi7h-rtrmadp-3-iran-crisis-oman-hormuz.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/19-2026-05-01t103455z-921331245-rc210la8oi7h-rtrmadp-3-iran-crisis-oman-hormuz.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48178510' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48178510' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.plough.com/en/topics/faith/discipleship/when-kierkegaard-got-cancelled' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mocked by a scandal sheet, Kierkegaard endured months of personal attacks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被丑闻媒体嘲讽，基尔凯郭尔经历数月的人身攻击</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 05:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48178510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48178510'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical piece recounts Kierkegaard enduring months of public harassment and ridicule in the press. It uses the episode to reflect on cancel culture, public scrutiny, and the resilience of intellectuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇历史回顾讲述基尔凯郭尔在大众媒体的审视下遭受数月的人身攻击与嘲弄。文本借此反思公众知识分子在媒体关注下的脆弱性，以及持续从事哲学研究所需的韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.plough.com/-/media/images/plough/article/2026/spring/goodman/goodmansocial.jpg?la=en' data-full='https://www.plough.com/-/media/images/plough/article/2026/spring/goodman/goodmansocial.jpg?la=en' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48157229' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48157229' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sites.math.rutgers.edu/~zeilberg/Opinion82.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Good Lemma Is Worth a Thousand Theorems (2007)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个好引理胜千个定理（2007）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 15, 2026 / 22:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48157229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48157229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48157229'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2007 piece argues that a single well-chosen lemma can unlock many theorems, underscoring the value of strategic insight over brute force in math. The article is aimed at mathematicians and students, explaining the craft of finding foundational results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2007 年的一篇文章主张，一条用得恰当的引理往往可以推导出大量定理，强调在数学中对策略性洞见的重视胜过纯粹的证明堆砌。文章面向数学家与学生，阐释发现基础结果的策略性价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48226703' data-tags='Culture,Design,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48226703' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thek6project.co.uk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Where are all the UK red telephone kiosks?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国红色电话亭都去哪儿了？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 11:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Veteran actor Michael Keating has died at 78. Known for his long-running work in science fiction roles and collaborations with fan-focused studios, his passing marks a loss for the broader community of genre media and audio-plays.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深演员迈克尔·基廷去世，享年 78 岁。他在科幻角色和与粉丝向工作室的长期合作中留下印记，其离世对科幻圈和相关有声作品的社区而言是一大损失。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(326) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/05/ebola-outbreak-now-third-largest-recorded-and-spreading-rapidly/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ebola Outbreak Now Third Largest Recorded and &quot;Spreading Rapidly&quot;</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 08:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48248659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48248659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48248659'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ebola outbreak is now the third largest on record and is spreading rapidly, raising alarms about surveillance, vaccine access, and international coordination. The situation underscores the ongoing global health stakes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>埃博拉疫情现已成为有记录以来第三大且扩散迅速，引发对监测、疫苗获取和国际协作的关注。强调全球卫生的紧急性与挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48183038' data-tags='DevOps,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48183038' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://modal.com/blog/truly-serverless-gpus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cutting inference cold starts by 40x with LP, FUSE, C/R, and CUDA-checkpoint</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 LP、FUSE、C/R 与 CUDA 检点将推理冷启动加速 40 倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 10:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183038' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183038'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modal claims to cut inference cold starts by 40x using a mix of persistence, layer fusion (FUSE), checkpoint/reload (C/R), and CUDA checkpointing. The approach reduces latency for serverless GPU inference, enabling faster bursts and lowering cost for sporadic workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Modal 声称通过持久化、层融合（FUSE）、检查点/重载（C/R）以及 CUDA 检点等方法，将推理冷启动时间缩短 40 倍。该方法降低了服务器端无服务器 GPU 推理的启动延迟，有助于应对突发工作负载并降低成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://modal.com/docs/social-image.png?title=Cutting+inference+cold+starts+by+40x+with+LP%2C+FUSE%2C+C%2FR%2C+and+cuda-checkpoint&amp;socialType=blog' data-full='https://modal.com/docs/social-image.png?title=Cutting+inference+cold+starts+by+40x+with+LP%2C+FUSE%2C+C%2FR%2C+and+cuda-checkpoint&amp;socialType=blog' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48177163' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48177163' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalitm.com/post/dont-answer-the-first-question/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t answer the first question</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要回答第一个问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 02:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48177163'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Don&#x27;t answer the first question argues for asking clarifying questions before answering to better understand the problem and provide higher-quality responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不要直接回答第一个问题，主张在回答前先提问澄清，以更好地理解问题并提供更高质量的解答。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48209105'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stable Audio 3 presents advances in diffusion-based audio synthesis, enabling high-fidelity, controllable sound generation. The work discusses architectural improvements, data considerations, and trade-offs between quality and latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stable Audio 3 在基于扩散的音频合成方面取得进展，使高保真、可控的音频生成成为可能。文中介绍了模型架构、数据选择以及质量与延迟之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48208685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48208685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48208685'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project exploring using AI agents to stress-test distributed systems, generate fault scenarios, and monitor observability. It demonstrates how AI can automate test case discovery and accelerate reliability improvements, while raising questions about reproducibility and trust in AI-driven tests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目探索利用 AI 代理人对分布式系统进行压力测试、场景生成和可观测性检查，展示 AI 在自动化测试用例发现与提升可靠性方面的潜力，同时也提出可重复性与信任问题。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48180172' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48180172' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deng-ai-lab/SFHformer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When Fast Fourier Transform Meets Transformer for Image Restoration (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FFT 与 Transformer 在图像修复中的结合（2024）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 07:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48180172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48180172' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48180172'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Combining FFT-based processing with Transformer architectures for image restoration, this work demonstrates efficient frequency-domain techniques that complement self-attention. The result is improved restoration quality on high-resolution images with practical inference considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该工作将 FFT 处理与 Transformer 架构结合用于图像修复，展示了在频域中的高效处理如何与自注意力互补，从而在高分辨率图像上提升重建质量，并兼顾推理时的实用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1e31ddc41fba5b72e8c7e048aa15ebbdc950f7b2da65e81fe230c19b8ca292a1/deng-ai-lab/SFHformer' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1e31ddc41fba5b72e8c7e048aa15ebbdc950f7b2da65e81fe230c19b8ca292a1/deng-ai-lab/SFHformer' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48208233' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48208233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1072657/394b87abd7cc215e/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swap tables, flash-friendly swap, swap_ops, and more</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>交换表、面向闪存的交换、swap_ops 等</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 07:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48208233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48208233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48208233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at kernel-level swap innovations including swap tables, flash-friendly swap, and swap_ops, aimed at improving paging performance on modern storage devices. The notes discuss how these features help latency and endurance in systems with large memory pressure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文概述内核层面的若干交换机制创新，包括交换表、面向闪存的交换和 swap_ops，旨在改善对现代存储设备的分页性能。介绍这些特性如何帮助降低延迟、提升在大内存压力场景下的耐久性和效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(336) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.psypost.org/tiktok-disproportionately-served-anti-democratic-videos-during-the-2024-election-study-finds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TikTok disproportionately served anti-Democratic videos during the 2024 election</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TikTok 在2024年大选期间不成比例地推送反民主视频</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 16:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48243021'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds TikTok delivered more anti-democratic content to users during the 2024 election, highlighting platform biases and challenges in moderating political content on short-video feeds. The results feed into policy debates about platform responsibility, algorithm transparency, and information ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现 TikTok 在 2024 年大选期间向用户投放更多反民主内容，凸显平台偏见以及在短视频信息流中对政治内容审核的挑战。此结果将推动关于平台责任、算法透明度和信息生态的政策讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://producelikeapro.com/blog/how-one-recording-mistake-created-a-musical-phenomenon-in-the-80s/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In 1979 engineer Hugh Padgham discovered &quot;gated reverb&quot; – by accident</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1979年，Hugh Padgham 意外发现了‘门控混响’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 09:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48170096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48170096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A studio accident led to the creation of gated reverb, a defining 80s effect that shaped pop and rock production. The piece traces how unintended mistakes in a vocal-gating technique yielded a new tool that influenced mixing, synths, and studio workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次录音现场的偶然失误催生了门控混响，成为80年代流行音乐的重要音效。文章回溯这一发现如何改变混音流程、合成器使用以及录音室的工作方式。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48199011'>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/scientists-id-corkscrew-killer-behind-gruesome-seal-deaths' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cannibalistic attacks between gray seals leave telltale “corkscrew” injuries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>灰海豹同类相食的螺旋形伤口</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 14, 2026 / 15:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48141960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48141960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48141960'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists have identified corkscrew-shaped injuries on gray seals caused by cannibalistic attacks, revealing a previously underappreciated behavior in crowded colonies. The findings shed light on predator-prey dynamics in crowded colonies and could inform conservation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在灰海豹身上发现螺旋形咬伤，证实同类之间的攻击行为。研究揭示了拥挤群落中的捕食者–猎物动力学，并可能为保育策略提供参考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
