<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>钱学森被囚禁：美国最大的战略错误</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 06:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48207251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48207251'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that imprisoning Qian Xuesen during the Cold War was a strategic misstep that delayed collaboration with a key aerospace talent and influenced tech leadership pole shifts. It frames the episode within policy decisions shaping tech power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在冷战时期对钱学森的监禁是美国的重大战略错误，错失与这位关键航天人才的合作机会，从而影响科技力量的领先格局。将此事件置于影响科技实力的政策议程中。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 05:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234831'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reveal the enzymatic steps tobacco plants use to synthesize nicotine, solving a two-century-old puzzle. The discovery opens doors for bioengineering nicotine pathways and advances plant biochemistry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究揭示烟草植物合成尼古丁的酶促步骤，解决了持续两百年的难题。此发现为尼古丁生物合成路径的生物工程应用以及植物生物化学研究带来新可能。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 09:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48195698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48195698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48195698'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go 1.24 enables serving HTTP/2 over cleartext (h2c), which can simplify certain internal deployments or proxy setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 1.24 现在支持在明文下提供 HTTP/2（H2C），适用于某些内部部署或代理场景。文中介绍如何在 Go 中开启 H2C，并讨论相关的安全性与兼容性权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative piece argues that AI&#x27;s true cost extends beyond dollars, including compute, data, energy, and risk, which can impede access. It advocates for efficiency, open models, or shared compute to democratize AI.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alignment pretraining: AI discourse creates self-fulfilling (mis)alignment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对齐预训练：AI 话语造成自我实现的对齐偏差</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48185938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that discourse around AI alignment can create feedback loops that yield self-fulfilling misalignment, and discusses how evaluation and incentives shape behavior during alignment pretraining.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为关于对齐的讨论可能产生循环反馈，导致自我实现的错配，并分析评估指标与激励机制如何在对齐预训练阶段塑造模型行为。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tritium.legal/blog/noroboto' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noroboto: Lying Fonts and Mitigation in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Noroboto：伪造字体及 Rust 的缓解措施</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 07:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48236791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48236791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48236791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Noroboto demonstrates how font files can lie about glyphs or metrics, creating security and usability risks in text rendering. The Rust implementation highlights safer parsers and mitigations to reduce memory-safety bugs and exploitation surface in font handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Noroboto 展示了字体文件如何在字形或度量方面作假，可能对文本渲染的安全和可用性造成风险。基于 Rust 的实现强调更安全的解析器和缓解措施，降低字体处理中的内存安全漏洞和利用面。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.codingexplorations.com/blog/understanding-singleflight-in-golang-a-solution-for-eliminating-redundant-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding Singleflight in Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>理解 Go 的 singleflight 机制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 16, 2026 / 14:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48164147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48164147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48164147'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines the Go singleflight pattern, which coalesces concurrent calls to avoid redundant work. The post explains how to use the concept to prevent cache stampedes, reduce I/O, and simplify code paths when multiple goroutines request the same resource. Includes practical examples and caveats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲解 Go 语言中的 singleflight 机制，即合并并发请求以避免重复工作。说明如何利用它防止缓存击穿、减少 I/O，并简化对同一资源的多路请求的代码路径。给出实际示例与注意事项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quuxplusone.github.io/blog/2021/02/15/devirtualization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When can the C++ compiler devirtualize a call?</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 01:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48167187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48167187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48167187'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the conditions under which a C++ compiler can devirtualize a call, enabling inlining and faster dispatch. It covers vtables, type information, and link-time optimizations that remove indirection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阐释了编译器在何种条件下能够对虚拟调用进行去虚拟化，以实现内联和更快的分派，并涉及虚表、类型信息及链接时优化等去除间接跳转的机制。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>霍尔木兹海峡关闭可能在一年内引发农业食品冲击与价格危机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 09:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48210450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48210450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FAO warns that closing Hormuz could trigger an agrifood price shock within a year, with knock-on effects for global supply chains. The piece frames geopolitical chokepoints as risk multipliers for food affordability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>粮农组织警告若霍尔木兹海峡关闭，可能在一年内引发农业食品价格冲击，冲击全球供应链和食品价格的可负担性。文章将地缘政治瓶颈视为食品成本的放大器。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryanjk5.github.io/posts/GOLDE/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simulating Infinity in Conway&#x27;s Game of Life with Modern C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用现代 C++ 在康威的生命游戏中模拟无限</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 11:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183563'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates how to model an unbounded universe in Conway&#x27;s Game of Life using modern C++, with data structures and algorithms that grow the grid as needed while keeping performance in check. The piece illustrates how infinity can be simulated within finite memory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示如何在康威生命游戏中使用现代 C++ 建模无穷大世界，采用可按需扩展的网格结构与高效算法实现。通过有限内存实现“无限”的仿真，便于理解相关算法与性能权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48245916' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ohwr.org/projects/white-rabbit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>White Rabbit – sub-nanosecond synchronization for large distributed systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>White Rabbit：大规模分布式系统的亚纳秒级同步</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 01:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48245916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48245916'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>White Rabbit is a precision time protocol extension that combines Synchronous Ethernet, PTP, and custom hardware to achieve sub-nanosecond synchronization across large networks. It enables deterministic timing for experiments and data centers, and demonstrates how specialized hardware-assisted timing outperforms standard clock synchronization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>White Rabbit 是一种将同步以太网、PTP与自研硬件结合的高精度时钟协议扩展，能够实现亚纳秒级的全网同步。它被用于需要确定性时序的大规模分布式系统和科研场景，显著优于常规的NTP/PTP 方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/ohwr/project/white-rabbit/-/wikis/uploads/48fce74397857b877007ae8de013c89b/wrNetwork2.jpg' data-full='https://gitlab.com/ohwr/project/white-rabbit/-/wikis/uploads/48fce74397857b877007ae8de013c89b/wrNetwork2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48211418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48211418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48211418'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Curly braces trace how block syntax influenced Unix tooling and C evolution, from early formatting choices to modern coding standards. The piece ties brace usage to scope management, macro behavior, and portability decisions across systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Curly braces 记录了大括号语法在 Unix 工具链和 C 语言演进中的作用，从早期格式化选择到现代编码规范。文章将大括号的使用与作用域管理、宏行为以及跨系统可移植性的决策联系起来。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/immigration/2026/05/22/new-rule-requires-most-green-card-applicants-apply-outside-us/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New rule requires most green-card applicants to apply from outside U.S.</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新规规定大多数绿卡申请者须在美国境外申请</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 20:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48244220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48244220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48244220'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new policy requires most green-card applicants to apply from outside the United States, shifting many processes abroad and potentially increasing processing times. This rule affects tech workers seeking permanent residency and could influence immigration patterns for U.S. tech companies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新政规定大多数绿卡申请者需在美国境外提交申请，许多流程因此转移到海外，可能增加处理时间。这一规则将影响寻求永久居留的科技工作者，并可能改变美国科技公司的人才引进与移民格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lanl.gov/media/publications/1663/science-of-unpredictability' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 1955 Los Alamos computer experiment changed our understanding of chaos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1955 年洛斯阿拉莫斯的计算机实验改变了我们对混沌的理解</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 09:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48195941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48195941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48195941'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historic experiment on a Los Alamos computer revealed chaotic behavior in a simple computation, underscoring sensitivity to initial conditions. The result helped shape modern chaos theory and influenced how scientists model unpredictability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项在洛斯阿拉莫斯进行的早期计算机实验揭示了简单程序中的混沌行为，强调对初始条件的敏感性。该结果推动了现代混沌理论的发展，并影响了科学家对不可预测性的建模方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RISC-V 在浮点运算实现上仍存在取舍，直接影响性能、编译器，以及生态系统的成熟度。文章梳理当前的实现状况、各方案的权衡，以及对未来硬件与软件协作的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Vim 中实现 Lisp（2019）展示了在编辑器中嵌入 Lisp解释器或相关特性的方法，体现了用 Lisp 宏和工具链扩展编辑器的思路。强调编辑器即可编程IDE的理念。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48240679' data-tags='Security,DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48240679' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-05-22-staged-publishing-and-new-install-time-controls-for-npm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Staged publishing and new install-time controls for npm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 推出分阶段发布和新的 NPM 安装时控件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 12:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48240679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48240679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48240679'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub introduces staged publishing for NPM and new install-time controls to reduce supply-chain risk, letting maintainers gate what users install and when. The changes help mitigate the blast radius of compromised packages and improve package hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 为 NPM 引入分阶段发布和安装时控件，以降低供应链风险，让维护者控制用户何时安装、安装哪些包。此举有助于减轻被恶意软件劫持的影响范围，并提升包的卫生性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/themes/github-2021-child/dist/img/social-v3-new-releases.jpg' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/themes/github-2021-child/dist/img/social-v3-new-releases.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48240331' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48240331' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/21/waymo-pauses-service-in-four-cities-as-robotaxis-keep-driving-into-floods/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo expands pause to four cities as robotaxis keep driving into floods</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo 将暂停扩至四个城市，机器人出租车在洪水中继续行驶</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 12:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48240331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48240331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48240331'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waymo paused service in four cities amid flood conditions, while robotaxi fleets continue to operate in others, highlighting safety and reliability tradeoffs in autonomous driving under extreme weather. The incident underscores the fragility of AI-driven transport and the need for robust disaster response and network resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo 在洪水条件下将服务暂停扩至四个城市，而在其他地区机器人出租车仍在运行，凸显极端天气下自动驾驶的安全性与可靠性权衡。此事凸显了 AI 驾驶运输的脆弱性，以及在灾害情境中需要更强的应急响应和网络韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/waymo-interior.jpg?resize=1200,802' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/waymo-interior.jpg?resize=1200,802' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.statnews.com/2026/05/22/seed-oils-healthy-fats-tallow-fact-check-cardiac-health/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The seed oil panic is hurting my cardiac patients</a></p>

The piece cautions clinicians and patients to rely on evidence-based fats and not succumb to fear-based trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇事实核查澄清关于籽油的错误信息，解释其对心血管健康的真实影响，并指出恐慌性饮食趋势可能带来更糟的选择。文章提醒医生与患者坚持循证营养，理性看待脂肪来源。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abuseofnotation.github.io/boolean-thinking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A case against Boolean logic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对布尔逻辑的质疑：一个案例</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 03:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case against Boolean logic argues for richer logics beyond true/false, highlighting practical limits of binary reasoning in programming, AI, and decision-making. The piece invites readers to consider multi-valued or probabilistic logics for nuanced systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对布尔逻辑的质疑提出超越真/假的更丰富逻辑，强调二元推理在编程、AI与决策中的实际局限。作者倡议在复杂系统中考虑多值逻辑或概率逻辑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://abuseofnotation.github.io/logo.svg' data-full='https://abuseofnotation.github.io/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbsnews.com/news/cbs-news-radio-last-day/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CBS Radio signs off after nearly 100 years of broadcasting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CBS 广播在近百年广播后正式停运</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 10:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48238817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48238817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CBS Radio signs off after nearly a century of broadcasting, marking an end of an era for legacy radio and a shift toward digital and podcast-oriented formats. The departure underscores how traditional media assets transition in a streaming-dominated era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在近百年的广播历史后，CBS 广播正式停运，标志着传统广播时代的落幕与向数字媒体的转型。此举反映出传统媒体资产在以流媒体为主的时代中的调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets2.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/05/20/a963538f-7176-423a-a00a-f63a8e9f2870/thumbnail/1200x630/bd162d06a286be4225ca4f346dc632d7/gettyimages-590994437.jpg' data-full='https://assets2.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/05/20/a963538f-7176-423a-a00a-f63a8e9f2870/thumbnail/1200x630/bd162d06a286be4225ca4f346dc632d7/gettyimages-590994437.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 22:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48203484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An reflective piece pondering uncertainty about the tech industry’s trajectory, citing uncertainty in AI, hardware, and policy. It argues for adaptable thinking and plural paths forward.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于科技行业未来方向不确定性的反思，讨论 AI、硬件与政策等因素的未知走向。主张采用更具适应性的思维和多元化的发展路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48226697' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48226697' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://82mhz.net/posts/2026/05/getting-an-old-computer-online-with-android-ethernet-tethering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Getting an old Computer online with Android Ethernet tethering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Android 以太网共享让旧电脑上网</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 11:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48226697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48226697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accessible guide shows how to bring an older computer online by using Android’s Ethernet tethering, turning a smartphone into a network gateway. It highlights low-cost ways to repurpose aging hardware and keep it useful in today’s networked world.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章演示了如何利用 Android 的以太网共享功能让一台老旧电脑接入互联网，将手机变成网络网关。强调低成本重用老设备、在当今网络环境中继续保持实用性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 21:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48189044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48189044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48189044'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Hsrs is a tool that generates type-safe bindings to bridge Haskell and Rust, reducing FFI pitfalls. It helps developers reuse Haskell logic from Rust apps while preserving type guarantees, reflecting ongoing language interoperability efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：Hsrs 是一个生成 Haskell 与 Rust 之间类型安全绑定的工具，降低外部语言接口的风险。它帮助开发者在 Rust 应用中安全调用 Haskell 代码，维持类型保证，体现跨语言互操作的持续进展。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Haiku OS 现已在 M1 Mac 上运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 11:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48183379' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48183379'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Haiku OS, a BeOS-inspired open-source OS, now runs on M1 Macs thanks to ARM64 progress. While still early, the port highlights ongoing hardware support efforts for niche operating systems and may attract hobbyists and developers exploring alternative Mac environments. Expect continued work on drivers, graphics, and system integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Haiku OS（BeOS 启发的开源系统）现已在 M1 Mac 上运行，得益于 ARM64 的进展。尽管仍处于早期阶段，这一端口展现了让小众操作系统兼容主流硬件的持续努力，可能吸引爱好者和开发者尝试替代型 Mac 环境。未来在驱动、图形与系统整合方面还将继续改进。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xint.io/blog/copy-fail-pod-to-host' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CopyFail: From Pod to Host</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CopyFail：从 Pod 到 Host</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 10:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48196337'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CopyFail delves into the pitfalls of copying data from a Kubernetes pod to the host. It explains frequent failure modes and practical remedies—covering permissions, mount configurations, and timing—aimed at making container-to-host data transfers more dependable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CopyFail 深入讲解在 Kubernetes 中从 Pod 复制数据到宿主机时常见的失败原因及对策，涵盖权限、挂载配置与时序等问题。给出可操作的修复思路，帮助开发者让容器与宿主机之间的数据传输更可靠。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post traces the origins of the infamous ERROR_ARENA_TRASHED code, explaining it as a relic from early heap-management concepts and how it tends to surface during memory corruption. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章追溯了‘ERROR_ARENA_TRASHED’ 错误代码的由来，指出它是早期堆管理理念的遗留，与内存损坏场景相关。文中给出开发者诊断根因和避免错误的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.luschny.de/math/factorial/FastFactorialFunctions.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fast Factorial Algorithms</a></p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article surveys methods for computing factorials faster than naive multiplication, including asymptotically efficient algorithms and practical optimizations. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>综述比朴素乘法更快的阶乘计算方法，包括渐进高效算法与实际优化，讨论权衡、精度问题以及阶乘在组合学、统计和密码学中的应用场景。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://microsandbox.dev/blog/oci-filesystem-47x-faster' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We made our filesystem 47× faster by deleting it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过删除它，我们的文件系统快了47倍</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 11:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48196831'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study where deleting filesystem components led to a 47x speedup, underscoring that sometimes simplifying the data path or metadata can yield dramatic performance gains. The lesson: not all optimization is about adding features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过删除某些文件系统组件实现 47 倍性能提升，强调简化数据路径和元数据有时比增加功能更有效。教训是优化不总是增添复杂性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of how AI and the rhetoric of humanity can shape public discourse, invoking a ‘Dr. Manhattan’ syndrome. It proposes interventions to calibrate AI storytelling and manage expectations around intelligence and agency.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vmax.ai/team/populora-co-evolving-llm-populations-for-reasoning-self-play' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PopuLoRA: Co-Evolving LLM Populations for Reasoning Self- Play</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PopuLoRA：用于推理自对弈的LLM族群共进化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 14:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48214188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48214188'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PopuLoRA demonstrates co-evolving populations of language models with self-play to boost reasoning capabilities without relying on a single, gigantic model. By fostering interaction among multiple agents, the approach explores emergent problem-solving and scalable AI alignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PopuLoRA 展示了通过自我对弈让一组语言模型共同进化，从而提升推理能力，而不依赖单一“大模型”。通过让多个代理相互作用，这一做法探索了涌现式问题解决和可扩展的 AI 对齐。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48175748' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48175748' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://museumplantinmoretus.be/en/worlds-two-oldest-printing-presses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The two oldest printing presses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>世界上最古老的两台印刷机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 17, 2026 / 22:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48175748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48175748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48175748'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The two oldest printing presses are preserved at the Plantin-Moretus Museum, offering tangible insight into early typography, craft, and the dissemination of knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>植物-莫雷图斯博物馆珍藏着两台最古老的印刷机，为早期字体排印、工艺与知识传播提供直观的材料证据。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48263253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seriot.ch/computation/jira.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jira Is Turing-Complete</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jira 具备图灵完备性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 20:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48263253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48263253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48263253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis shows Jira&#x27;s workflow language is Turing-complete, meaning it can simulate any computation given enough resources. This raises questions about the line between powerful automation and potential security risks or runaway resource use in project-management tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项分析表明 Jira 的工作流语言具备图灵完备性，理论上可以在资源充足时模拟任意计算。这让人重新思考在项目管理工具中对自动化能力的界限，以及潜在的安全风险或资源耗尽问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://seriot.ch/resources/jira_tc/addition_rules.png' data-full='https://seriot.ch/resources/jira_tc/addition_rules.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/931532/bambu-agpl-pawel-jarczak-open-source-threat-dmca-github' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Fuck you, Bambu&#x27;: How one private message could change the face of 3D printing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“去你们的，Bambu”：一条私信如何改变 3D 打印领域</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 21:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48254345'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A private message incident reveals tensions around AGPL licensing and DMCA enforcement in 3D printing circles; the fallout could shift how developers and vendors negotiate open-source terms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起私信事件揭示了在 3D 打印领域关于 AGPL 许可与 DMCA 执行的紧张关系，事件的后续可能改变开发者与厂商在开源条件上的谈判方式。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48185778' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/05/18/us-bill-would-overcharge-evs-to-pay-for-road-damage-they-arent-doing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US bill proposes new national EV tax, while some push to slash gas tax to zero</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国拟定新国家级电动车税，同时有人主张将燃油税降为零</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 14:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48185778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48185778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48185778'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US bill proposes new national EV tax, while some push to slash gas tax to zero. The debate has implications for EV pricing, road funding, and political viability, and may spur interest in alternatives like a vehicle-miles-traveled tax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道美国一项提议中的国家级电动车税以弥补燃油税减少带来的财政缺口，同时也有声音主张将燃油税降为零。此举影响电动车价格、道路资金与政策走向，或促使人们考虑如按里程计税等替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2024/04/Capitol_Building_Full_View-public-domain-e1713472940371.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2024/04/Capitol_Building_Full_View-public-domain-e1713472940371.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48234391' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48234391' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arnon.dk/the-current-ai-pricing-was-always-going-to-go-away/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The current AI pricing was always going to go away</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现有的AI定价注定要消失</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 04:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The current AI pricing model was always temporary as realities of compute costs and competition push prices toward commoditized, usage-based schemes. The piece anticipates a shift away from flat-rate or subsidized access toward more dynamic, market-driven pricing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现有的AI定价模式本来就只是过渡期，随着算力成本与竞争加剧，价格将走向通用化、基于用量的定价。文章预见价格将从固定或补贴式向更动态、市场驱动的定价转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arnon.dk/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/image-scaled-1-scaled.png' data-full='https://arnon.dk/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/image-scaled-1-scaled.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://meertens.dev/blog/github-enables-domain-abuse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My domain got abused on GitHub Pages</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 00:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48190348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48190348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48190348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study of domain abuse enabled via GitHub Pages, describing how misconfigurations or policy gaps allowed attackers to host or impersonate domains, with lessons on security and mitigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享了在 GitHub Pages 上域名滥用的案例，分析了配置和策略缺口如何被利用，以及对安全和防护的启示与对策。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48243666' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48243666' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC1360393/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Didgeridoo playing as alternative treatment for obstructive sleep apnea(2006)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>吹迪吉里杜管作为阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停的替代治疗（2006）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 18:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48243666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48243666'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2006 study reports that regular didgeridoo playing may reduce sleep apnea severity and daytime sleepiness, presumably by strengthening pharyngeal muscles and improving airway stability. The findings support a growing body of evidence that simple, non-invasive oropharyngeal training can help manage obstructive sleep apnea.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2006年的一项研究显示，规律练习迪吉里杜管可能降低阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停的严重度，并改善白天嗜睡，可能通过强化咽部肌肉与改善气道稳定性实现。这为长期、低成本的咽部训练作为非药物治疗提供了证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/pd-medc-pmc-cloudpmc-viewer/production/674d4f95/var/data/static/img/us_flag.svg' data-full='https://cdn.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/pmc/pd-medc-pmc-cloudpmc-viewer/production/674d4f95/var/data/static/img/us_flag.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes fragmentation across code-forging ecosystems and argues for better interoperability, standard formats, and tooling. It offers guidance for developers on navigating multiple forges and migrating dependencies.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软的6502 BASIC现开源（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 06:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48257058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48257058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48257058'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft has open-sourced its historic 6502 BASIC interpreter, a relic from early home computing. The move highlights a broader trend of preserving legacy software as open source and gives hobbyists a clean reference implementation to study or adapt.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软已将其历史悠久的6502 BASIC解释器开源，这是早期家用计算机的里程碑。此举反映了保护遗留软件的开源趋势，为爱好者提供了一个整洁的参考实现，便于研究或改造。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48196777' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48196777' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(439) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/books/2026/may/18/big-tech-monopolies-democracy-mordecai-kurz' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Capitalism has to become more humane&#x27;: a Stanford economist on big tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《资本主义必须更具人性》：斯坦福经济学家谈大科技</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 10:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48196777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48196777'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Stanford economist argues capitalism should be more humane amid big tech, calling for reforms to curb monopoly power and protect democratic institutions. The piece frames tech as a policy and economic issue, not just a market normality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福经济学家在讨论大科技时主张让资本主义更具人性，呼吁通过改革来限制垄断势力并保护民主制度。该观点将科技视为政策与经济问题，而不仅仅是市场现象。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/9479520df4aec8ecdfef625604cd75395ae21889/335_0_3518_2814/master/3518.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=255c26cdd273cf4485b0beb79e49dc96' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/9479520df4aec8ecdfef625604cd75395ae21889/335_0_3518_2814/master/3518.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=255c26cdd273cf4485b0beb79e49dc96' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48236057' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48236057' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(441) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boostedcpp.net/2026/05/18/neoclassical-c-segmented-iterators-revisited-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neoclassical C++: segmented iterators revisited</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新古典 C++：再谈分段迭代器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 07:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48236057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48236057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48236057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisits segmented iterators in C++, focusing on data locality and iterator semantics. It discusses practical implications for library authors and performance, including how to compose segmented iterators with standard algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>重新探讨 C++ 的分段迭代器，聚焦数据局部性与迭代语义的实践意义。讨论对库作者的实际影响与性能取舍，以及如何将分段迭代器与标准算法更高效地组合使用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48259158' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://christopherkrapu.com/blog/2026/dont-know-where-your-data-is-from/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t know where your data is from? Bayesian modeling for unknown coordinates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不知道数据来自何处？未知坐标的贝叶斯建模</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 10:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48259158'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>When data provenance is uncertain or coordinates are missing, Bayesian modeling can quantify uncertainty and help infer likely origins, enabling better data integration and bias assessment. The post outlines practical approaches and cautions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当数据来源不确定或坐标信息缺失时，贝叶斯建模可量化不确定性并推断可能的来源，帮助实现更可靠的数据整合与偏差评估。文中介绍了实用的方法与注意事项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://christopherkrapu.com/images/2026-05-24-dont-know-where-your-data-is-from/error-in-location-grid.png' data-full='https://christopherkrapu.com/images/2026-05-24-dont-know-where-your-data-is-from/error-in-location-grid.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>域名伪装注入攻击在多代理大语言模型系统中规避检测</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 11:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48239786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48239786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show domain-camouflaged injection attacks that slip past detectors in multi-agent LLM environments, revealing new attack surfaces when agents collaborate. The finding calls for stronger defense-in-depth, improved prompt provenance, and better monitoring of model interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示了域名伪装注入攻击，能够在多代理大语言模型系统中绕过检测，暴露协同工作时的新攻击面。此发现促使加强防御深度、改进提示溯源与对模型交互的监控措施。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48242957' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-tobacco-psychedelics-psilocybin-dmt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tobacco plant can produce five psychedelics, including psilocybin and DMT</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>烟草植物可生产五种致幻物质，包括裸盖菇素和 DMT</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 16:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48242957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48242957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48242957'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers engineered tobacco plants to biosynthesize five psychedelics, including psilocybin and DMT, offering a potentially scalable route to production. The development raises biosafety, ethics, and regulatory questions about plant-based manufacturing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员对烟草植物进行改造，使其能够生物合成五种致幻物质（包括裸盖菇素和 DMT），这为大规模生产提供了潜在路径。此项发展引发生物安全、伦理和监管方面的讨论，涉及植物制造的合规性与风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/one-plant-engineered-t.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/one-plant-engineered-t.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48177891' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thebowesmuseum.org.uk/collections/the-silver-swan/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Silver Swan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>银天鹅</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 04:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48177891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48177891'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Silver Swan is a renowned 17th-century silver automaton on display at The Bowes Museum, celebrated for its clockwork engineering and exceptional craftsmanship. It stands as a marquee example of early mechanical artistry and decorative arts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>银天鹅是大博物馆收藏的著名17世纪银质自动人偶，以精湛的齿轮机构和工艺水平著称，是早期机械艺术与装饰工艺的代表性珍品。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thebowesmuseum.org.uk/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/The-Swan-942-2-cropped-portrait-scaled.webp' data-full='https://thebowesmuseum.org.uk/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/The-Swan-942-2-cropped-portrait-scaled.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vostride/id-agent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Id-agent – Token efficient UUID alternative for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Id-agent：面向 AI 代理的令牌高效 UUID 替代方案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 04:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48191852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48191852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48191852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Id-agent introduces a token-efficient UUID alternative designed for AI agents, reducing token overhead and enabling scalable, low-latency agent identification across sessions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Id-agent 提供一种面向 AI 代理的令牌高效 UUID 替代方案，降低 token 开销，实现跨会话的可扩展、低延迟标识。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48228608' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48228608' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lospino.so/blog/c-constructs-that-still-dont-work-in-cpp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C constructs that still don&#x27;t work in C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 C++ 中仍然不可用的 C 构造</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 13:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48228608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48228608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48228608'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article catalogs C language constructs that fail to compile or behave differently in C++, highlighting the gaps between C and C++ language compatibility. It warns developers blending C and C++ about pitfalls and which features remain non-portable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中列出了一些在 C 语言中可用、但在 C++ 中仍无法正常工作的构造，揭示了两种语言在语法与语义上的差异。对于在 C 代码混合到 C++ 项目中的开发者，这些坑点尤为重要，指出哪些特性仍未实现跨语言的一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lospino.so/images/lospino-trail.png' data-full='https://lospino.so/images/lospino-trail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN showcases Hugo Flow, a Git-backed front-end for the Hugo static site generator. It enables content authors to manage posts and edits through versioned Git workflows, lowering collaboration friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了 Hugo Flow——一个基于 Git 的 Hugo 静态站点前端。它让内容作者通过版本化的 Git 工作流来管理文章与编辑，降低协作摩擦。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://punchcards.tristandavey.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tristan Davey&#x27;s Punch Card Archive</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 07:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48193932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48193932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48193932'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tristan Davey’s Punch Card Archive curates scans and metadata from vintage punched cards, preserving early computing history for researchers and enthusiasts. The collection offers a tangible window into how programs were stored and manipulated before modern digitization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tristan Davey 的打孔卡档案收集并整理了早期 punched cards 的扫描件与元数据，为研究者和爱好者保留了珍贵的计算史。该收藏提供了一个直观的窗口，展示在数字化普及前程序如何被存储和操作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://punchcards.tristandavey.com/images/heroes/index.jpg' data-full='https://punchcards.tristandavey.com/images/heroes/index.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/cars/2026/05/bipartisan-bill-in-congress-includes-130-annual-ev-registration-fee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US transportation bill would add a $130 annual fee for EV drivers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国交通法案拟为电动车车主新增每年130美元费用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 13:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48198659'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bipartisan bill would impose a $130 annual registration fee on EVs, potentially financing infrastructure but also impacting total ownership costs. It highlights the policy trade-offs in accelerating electrification while funding roads and networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项两党法案拟对电动车征收每年 130 美元的注册费，旨在为基础设施筹资，同时影响车主的总体拥有成本，凸显在推进电动化的同时对路网等的资金分配权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2201006794-1-2048x1365.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2201006794-1-2048x1365.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 20, 2026 / 13:21 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48198293' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48198293' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.google/blog/empirical-research-assistance-era-from-nature-publication-to-catalyzing-computational-discovery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Era: From Nature publication to catalyzing Computational Discovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Era：从 Nature 发表到催化计算发现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 19, 2026 / 12:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48198293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48198293'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Era expands from a Nature publication into an effort to accelerate computational discovery, helping researchers translate findings into AI-assisted workflows and scalable experiments. It showcases AI-assisted empirical research support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Era 不再局限于一篇 Nature 论文，而是推动计算发现的催化过程，帮助研究者将发现转化为 AI 辅助的工作流与可扩展的实验。它展示了对经验研究的 AI 支持。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1985 年的一篇论文提出编程是一种理论建构的过程，通过代码实验来提出和验证计算假设，影响了我们对软件工程与教育的理解。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 18, 2026 / 08:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48181132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48181132'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hiring approach that ships to production: replacing whiteboard questions with pull requests. Candidates contribute real code, and reviews influence the hiring decision, reducing guesswork and misalignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种招聘新思路：把面试直接推向生产，用 pull request 来评估候选人。候选人提交实际代码，评审过程成为招聘决策的一部分，降低了凭空推测的偏差。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/electronics/comments/1td7yxl/i_built_a_fully_selfpowered_computer_in_actual/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A self-powered computer in actual credit-card size (~1mm thick)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自供电、信用卡大小的计算机（约1毫米厚）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 14:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48251528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48251528' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48251528'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fully self-powered computer the size of a credit card (about 1mm thick) demonstrates advances in energy harvesting and ultra-low-power design. It hints at new forms of portable, maintenance-free devices for IoT and edge computing, though practical use cases and reliability remain questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一台真正自供电、接近信用卡大小、厚度约1毫米的计算机展示了能量采集与极低功耗设计的进展。它为物联网和边缘计算带来潜在“免维护”的新型设备，但实际应用场景与可靠性仍待解决。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Evaluating SPEC CPU2026 compares modern CPUs under industry-standard workloads, highlighting trends in compiler optimization and architectural performance. The results help developers and buyers gauge real-world efficiency beyond clock speeds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 SPEC CPU2026 的评测揭示现代处理器在标准工作负载下的表现趋势，强调编译器优化与体系结构性能的重要性。结果帮助开发者和买家在超算/商用场景中做出更贴近现实的判断。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitander.xyz/posts/buildcraft-is-a-compiler-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Buildcraft Is a Compiler Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Buildcraft 是一个编译器问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 02:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48233829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48233829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48233829'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that Buildcraft&#x27;s core hurdles stem from treating build processes as a compiler-like problem, suggesting a rethink of tooling around dependency analysis, incremental builds, and reproducible pipelines. Provides actionable insights for developers designing build systems or language tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为 Buildcraft 的核心难题源于把构建过程当作类似编译器的问题来设计，提出重新思考依赖分析、增量构建与可重复流水线等工具方法。为设计构建系统或语言工具的开发者提供可操作的启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 11:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48227068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48227068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netflix video traffic at 400 Gb/s and beyond—presented in a 2022 summit PDF—explores network architecture, caching strategies, and streaming reliability at scale. The document offers historical context for current CDN and ISP capacity planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2022 年的研讨会报告披露了 Netflix 视频流量在 400Gb/s及以上速率下的网络架构、缓存策略与大规模流媒体的稳定性要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48221568' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twixt.games/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Twixt – transform one word into another in four moves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Twixt——在四步中把一个单词变成另一个单词</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 05:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48221568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48221568'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents Twixt, a web app that turns one word into another in exactly four moves, likely using a dictionary graph and BFS search. It showcases a playful demonstration of pathfinding in word graphs and an accessible interface for exploring word relations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示 Twixt，一款网页应用在恰好四步内将一个单词变成另一个单词，可能基于词典图与广度优先搜索实现路径。展示了在词汇图上的路径搜索以及易于使用的界面。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(474) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielmiessler.com/blog/companies-graph-of-algorithms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Companies Are Just a Graph of Algorithms</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 21:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48263284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48263284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48263284'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern companies are largely shaped by algorithms and data pipelines, effectively forming a graph of interconnected decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代公司在很大程度上由算法和数据管线驱动，形成一张相互连接的决策图。文章探讨这一视角对透明度、治理以及自动化组织中人类角色演变的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48259784' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hollandtech.net/claude-is-not-your-architect/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Is Not Your Architect. Stop Letting It Pretend</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 不是你的架构师；别让它假装</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 11:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48259784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48259784'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude is not a substitute for a software architect; overreliance on generative AI for architecture decisions can lead to brittle designs and misalignments with real-world constraints. The piece argues for keeping humans in the loop and validating architecture choices with domain expertise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 不能替代软件架构师；过度依赖生成式 AI 做架构决策可能导致设计脆弱、与实际约束不符。文章主张让人类保持决策主导并用领域专业知识对架构方案进行核验。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theurbanist.org/west-coast-cities-turn-to-vacancy-taxes-to-grapple-with-housing-crisis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>West Coast Cities Turn to Vacancy Taxes to Grapple with Housing Crisis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西海岸城市通过空置税应对住房危机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 24, 2026 / 12:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48260448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48260448' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48260448'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>West Coast cities are experimenting with vacancy taxes to curb housing shortages, discouraging owners from leaving units empty and funding housing initiatives. The move reflects a broader strategy to boost housing supply and address affordability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西海岸多城正在试验空置税以缓解住房短缺，打击房屋空置并为住房计划筹集资金。这一举措体现了扩大住房供给和解决可负担性问题的综合策略。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Spec-Driven Development Workflow for Claude Code</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 21, 2026 / 20:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48231575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48231575'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces a spec-driven workflow for Claude Code, where developers write formal specs and Claude Code produces code, tests, and iterations aligned to the spec. The approach aims to improve reliability and reduce debugging time in software development with AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了一种基于规范的 Claude Code 开发工作流：先写正式规范，再由 Claude Code 按规范生成代码、测试与迭代。该方法旨在提升软件开发的可靠性并降低调试时间。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48254993' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/local-llm-clarifying-questions-system-prompt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local LLMs perform better when you teach them to ask before they answer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地化大型语言模型在被教会先问问题再回答时表现更佳</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 23, 2026 / 23:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48254993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48254993'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that prompting local LLMs to ask clarifying questions before answering improves accuracy and robustness, reducing misinterpretations. This supports adopting a clarifying-question stance as a standard system prompt for offline setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出本地化的大型语言模型在被指示先提问再回答时，准确性与鲁棒性有所提升，能减少误解。这支持在离线环境中把澄清性提问作为系统提示的常规做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/04/raspberry-pi-llama-cpp-3.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/04/raspberry-pi-llama-cpp-3.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.salmonfinder.com/2026/05/13/bristol-bay-kodiak-predictions-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Predicting the 2026 Bristol Bay and Kodiak Salmon Runs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>预测2026年布里斯托湾与科迪亚克鲑鱼回游</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: May 22, 2026 / 05:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48234793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48234793'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forecasts for the 2026 Bristol Bay and Kodiak salmon runs weigh environmental signals, climate variables, and fishery models to estimate run size. The predictions inform fisheries management and sustainable harvest planning, while noting uncertainty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对2026年布里斯托湾和科迪亚克的鲑鱼回游进行预测，综合环境信号、气候变量和渔业模型来估计产量规模。预测为渔业管理和可持续捕捞计划提供依据，同时承认存在不确定性。</p>
